## Diffusion / 生成式AI

### 1. [AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：video generation, camera-controlled video, diffusion, generator for on-the-fly camera, on-the-fly camera control. BOTH, few-step generator for on-the-fly
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944v1>

**原始摘要**

Camera-controlled video generation re-renders a video along a target camera trajectory, yet moving the camera around an object, the most natural way to change the viewpoint of an object-centric scene, remains difficult. Existing methods condition either on camera parameters, which are scale-ambiguous in monocular video, or on explicit geometry such as warped point clouds or depth, which breaks down under large viewpoint changes. We present AnyCam, a method that represents the camera with an anchor object in the scene: we reconstruct it in 3D and render it along the target trajectory, a camera signal that is scale-aware, view-complete and easy to specify relative to the object. A video diffusion model transfers the content and dynamics of the source video to the new viewpoint. Its H-Concat layout aligns source and target frames in time, so the model can be distilled into AnyCam-AR, a streaming few-step generator for on-the-fly camera control. Both models are trained on paired videos with pixel-aligned anchor renderings from multi-camera videos and static-scene captures. Experiments on diverse datasets and trajectories show that AnyCam performs favorably against camera-controlled video generation and 3D reconstruction methods in camera control and visual quality.

### 2. [Improving and Expanding Video Editing Capabilities Without Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34941v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：video editor, video editing, editing capabilities, image editing, i2vt improves existing editing, BOTH openve-bench and openve++.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34941v1>

**原始摘要**

Image editing models can follow a wide range of instructions, but video editing models still lag behind, limited in part by the difficulty of collecting paired source and edited videos. Is it possible to transfer advanced image editing capabilities to video editors without new paired videos? We introduce Image-to-Video Transfer (I2VT), which improves pretrained video editors and expands them to new editing tasks using an image editor. It enables efficient post-training with no architectural changes and no new videos, using only individual images. Our key idea is to let the video editor role-play as an image editor: it edits an image as a one-frame video, with the image editor guiding the video editor's predictions at each denoising step. Although the video editor sees only single images during training, at test time it applies the improved and expanded capabilities to whole video clips while preserving temporal consistency. To push the boundaries of video editing, we introduce OpenVE++, an extension of OpenVE-Bench that adds four new editing tasks useful for video production, including action changes, expression changes, body movements, and scene relighting. Our experiments across three different video editors show that I2VT improves existing editing capabilities and expands them to new tasks, and our models achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on both OpenVE-Bench and OpenVE++.

### 3. [LuxAvatar: Real-Time Relightable Head Avatars via Causal Neural Shading of 3D Intrinsic Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal autoregressive neural, neural shader, diffusion, fine-tuning, distillation, framework for real-time HEAD
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940v1>

**原始摘要**

We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers. Conditioned on these buffers, our neural shader formulates forward rendering as a conditional video diffusion process, bypassing explicit PBR computation to synthesize realistic shading effects under target illumination. We achieve real-time streaming through heterogeneous distillation from a pretrained multi-step bidirectional relighting teacher to a two-step causal autoregressive neural shader, inheriting broad synthetic lighting priors while minimizing conditioning and decoding latency. To further specialize these priors to human heads, we construct a hybrid relighting dataset for domain-specific fine-tuning, capturing authentic facial dynamics and physically grounded light transport. Extensive experiments show that our framework enables 3D-controllable, real-time portrait animation and relighting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity, motion control, and lighting accuracy.

### 4. [A Mesh for Every Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：MESH for every, benchmark, dynamic gaussian splatting methods, every frame. existing dynamic, keeps every gaussian, every moment
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939v1>

**原始摘要**

We present MEMO (a Mesh for Every MOment), a method that reconstructs the geometry of complete dynamic scenes, captured by multiple synchronized cameras, and extracts a mesh at every frame. Existing dynamic Gaussian splatting methods either model appearance alone or target isolated objects segmented by foreground masks, and all of them recover meshes by TSDF fusion or Poisson reconstruction, whose fixed grids limit the detail they can capture. At the core of MEMO is a per-Gaussian motion model inspired by Gaussian processes, which represents each Gaussian's position and rotation over time as a combination of temporal Gaussian kernels. The model keeps every Gaussian a valid surface element throughout the sequence and carries an implicit representation of the dynamic geometry, so a mesh can be derived at any time from a field defined by the Gaussians. Although common in real captures, large and fast motion poses a challenge that dynamic Gaussian splatting methods usually overlook. To account for it, we design a track-based initialization scheme that fits each Gaussian's initial trajectory to point tracks in closed form. We evaluate MEMO on Blender-Moments, a new benchmark of nine animated scenes with ground-truth meshes at every frame, and on two real-world datasets. MEMO reconstructs the most accurate geometry among state-of-the-art methods, and remains competitive in novel-view synthesis and training time. Project page: https://memo-4d.github.io

### 5. [Can image generation help visual reasoning? An empirical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34935v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reasoning, multimodal, vision-language, repeated image edits yield, image generation HELP visual, pairs generation lowers MEAN
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34935v1>

**原始摘要**

Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. A model describes the helper it needs and then answers using the original and generated images together. Across 13 vision-language model and generator pairs, generation lowers mean accuracy by 3.7 percentage points, with declines on nine of ten tasks. Two unified models, BAGEL and Janus-Pro-7B, each lose accuracy on eight tasks. On VSTAR, adding a generated image to the same planning hint lowers accuracy by 10.47 points. Repeated image edits yield different outcomes across tasks. ChartQAPro and MathVista stay below their no-generation baselines, while ERQA gains three points after ten edits.

### 6. [MERLoc: Robust Robot Localization in Dynamic Environments with Pose-Based Retrieval over 3D Gaussian Splatting References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34932v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pose-based retrieval, gaussian splatting, REAL dynamic scenes pose-based, dynamic environments with pose-based, space for the robot, views are sampled OVER
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34932v1>

**原始摘要**

Robots operating in previously mapped spaces must localize accurately despite moving people and rearranged objects. Most localization pipelines match the query against a sparse set of captured reference images retrieved by appearance, which is brittle under scene change and repeating structures. We present MERLoc, which turns a single metric 3D Gaussian Splatting scan into a dense Functional Reference Memory. Reference views are sampled over the navigable space for the robot's morphology and sensors at a target density and rendered offline. Pre-encoding them for the chosen off-the-shelf localizer removes the reference encoder from inference. A pose-based retrieval strategy then selects references by spatial proximity and viewing direction rather than appearance, sidestepping visual ambiguities whenever a prior exists. Across one classical and four learned localizers in simulated and real dynamic scenes, pose-based retrieval matches or outperforms appearance retrieval in most cases, even when the latter is gated by the same prior. On real data, it lowers position RMSE by 15-60 % in nine of ten backend/split combinations, and the rendered memory outperforms real-image caches posed by Structure-from-Motion. The system runs onboard a Jetson Orin NX at 5 Hz, enabling a navigation policy to successfully traverse a hallway with people roaming and rearranging objects.

### 7. [High-Fidelity Image Matting with Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：high-fidelity image, pixel-space diffusion, image matting, fine-tuning, text-to-image, mismatch with the high-frequency
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929v1>

**原始摘要**

Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone's native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. With pixel-accurate synthetic supervision and real-image fine-tuning, PixelMatte predicts alpha mattes in a single forward pass. Two lightweight calibration modules, LayerShift and High-Pass Refiner, further improve regional opacity accuracy and boundary fidelity. Experiments across four benchmarks and two distinct pixel diffusion backbones demonstrate substantial improvements over existing methods, reducing SAD and gradient errors by 24-44% relative to RenderMatte on P3M-500-NP and AM-2K, while achieving competitive performance on AIM-500 and RenderMatte-2K. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: https://white-yc.github.io/pixelmatte/.

### 8. [Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, fine-tuning, vision-language, VLAS are capable low-level, keeps the VLA frozen, VLA frozen and steers
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914v1>

**原始摘要**

Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA's intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA's denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object's poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA's weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen π0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a π0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID π0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm's reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

### 9. [LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal window attention, video VAE, diffusion, distillation, minimax-h3 livevae achieves, THAN the official VAE
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912v1>

**原始摘要**

While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

### 10. [Agentic LongLive: Asynchronous Agentic Memory for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agentic longlive, memory for LONG video, agentic memory for LONG, LONG video generation., asynchronous agentic memory, reinforcement learning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907v1>

**原始摘要**

Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. From this entity memory it composes a global image and a per-shot memory image, which an auxiliary GPU prefills into the generator's attention sink before the next shot starts, and writes entity cards for the prompts that name them, so the video never waits for memory and video errors are not written back. A small language model, trained with reinforcement learning and an image reward calibrated using downstream video outcomes, decides what to remember before the next prompt is known. On LongStory-Online, a new benchmark of 25 stories (857 shots) revealed one shot at a time, Agentic LongLive improves persistent-state accuracy of LongLive-2.0, its base generator, by 26.3 points, protagonist identity by 44.9, and revisited-place consistency by 40.4, at about 1% extra generation time on H100. The full system streams faster than real time on one 8xB200 node with the generator in BF16 (34.3 FPS), keeps image quality stable over 14-minute stories, and improves identity on two MiniMax-H3 generators, one of which, LongLive-H3, also runs in real time with it. To our knowledge, it is the first real-time video generator with text and image memory kept by an LLM agent.

### 11. [Towards Closing the Performance Gap in Cross-Modal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：place recognition, cross-modal place, attention, fine-tuning, text-to-image, vision-language
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877v1>

**原始摘要**

Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. Stage 1 performs global retrieval by pairing a jointly aligned DINOv2-B/14 visual backbone with a progressively fine-tuned BGE-Large text encoder, while Stage 2 refines top candidates using a lightweight token-level cross-attention reranker. Through systematic analysis, we isolate the drivers of retrieval accuracy and show that visual token density provides the largest performance improvement, while text encoder capacity and task fine-tuning offer complementary gains. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. Code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/oferidan1/CrossPlace

### 12. [Probing Visual Idiosyncrasies in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34843v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：visual idiosyncrasies, text-to-image, t2i, source and which visual, leading models NOW produce, points. these idiosyncrasies MAKE
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34843v1>

**原始摘要**

Text-to-Image (T2I) models turn short text descriptions into images, and the leading models now produce images of comparable visual quality. Whether their outputs still carry model-specific characteristics matters for attribution, for model comparison beyond aggregate quality, and for evaluations that assume anonymity. We ask whether images from frontier T2I models can still be classified by source, and which visual properties make this possible. Here we show that a standard ResNet-50 identifies the source of an image among HiDream-O1-Dev, FLUX.2-dev, HunyuanImage-3.0, and Qwen-Image-2512, the four highest-ranked open-source models on the Text-to-Image Arena, with 100.00% accuracy on held-out prompts. Human participants reach only 62.61% on the pairwise version of this task, so the distinguishing cues are not visually obvious. They are also not tied to a particular classifier and survive severe image corruptions and out-of-distribution prompts. We trace them to visual idiosyncrasies at three levels. Low-level texture statistics alone let a lightweight multi-layer perceptron match ResNet-50. Object layout, depth, and albedo estimated from the images each carry a source signal. Image captions reveal model-specific preferences in style and background that the prompts never specify. A one-sentence hint about these cues raises human accuracy by 9.03 percentage points. These idiosyncrasies make source classification a tool for measuring similarity between models and between real and generated images, and they caution against assuming anonymity in human preference evaluation of T2I models.

### 13. [Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：alignment, benchmark, diffusion, text-to-image, editing and introduce seam-bench, image editing and introduce
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839v1>

**原始摘要**

Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

### 14. [Not all world models can doubt: how training objective and architecture shape epistemic uncertainty in JEPA and RSSM world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：epistemic uncertainty, world models, shape epistemic, last-layer laplace and ensembles, recurrent state-space model RSSM, uncertainty-penalised planning the RSSM
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830v1>

**原始摘要**

World models predict how an environment responds to actions, so that candidate actions can be evaluated before execution. Executed actions, or decisions, taken in this manner are only as reliable as the model's predictive capabilities, and predictions in turn are only reliable within the model's experience. Epistemic uncertainty (EU) is the signal that marks where that experience ends. In this work we offer an extensive empirical study on how training objectives and architectures shape epistemic uncertainty of world models. More precisely, we present a controlled comparison of post-hoc EU (last-layer Laplace and ensembles) between two dominant models of different paradigms: 1) LeWorldModel (LeWM): A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), which predicts the next embedding without a decoder, and 2) DreamerV3: A recurrent state-space model (RSSM), which reconstructs the observation from a recurrent latent state. In ten simulated visual-control environments, we follow a controlled state perturbation of the environment state through every stage of the models up to the final EU estimate. We find that the RSSM attenuates the state perturbation through two mechanisms before it reaches an estimator on the predictor feature. Its recurrent update averages over context, so a change in a single frame reaches the state at a fraction of its size and recovers only as the change persists. Its reconstruction objective weights an entity by the pixels it covers, so a small object can be lost due to the training objective. The JEPA has neither mechanism and passes the change through all stages to the uncertainty. We further show that this translates to gaps in five uncertainty related downstream tasks, from unfamiliar-state detection to uncertainty-penalised planning: the RSSM uncertainty misses deviations that its own posterior registers, whereas the JEPA uncertainty reports what its model sees.

### 15. [Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：visual reasoning, video generation, agent, THAN video, rather THAN, visual reasoners
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813v1>

**原始摘要**

Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

### 16. [HOG-WAM: Improving Visual Robustness through Future Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：attention, diffusion, transformer, hog-wam which ADDS future, hog-wam achieves the highest, introduce hog-wam which ADDS
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811v1>

**原始摘要**

World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM's RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/HOG-WAM.

### 17. [WorldExpand: Building Coherent 3D Worlds via Sparse Joint Panoramic Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：joint panoramic, attention, diffusion, in-the-wild scenes. worldexpand RUNS, diverse in-the-wild scenes. worldexpand, sparse complementary and high-resolution
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810v1>

**原始摘要**

Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. This representation concentrates the generation budget on informative scene regions and preserves the fine-grained details required for 3D construction. WorldExpand addresses two key challenges: where to observe and how to synthesize. Our Coverage-Guided View Selection identifies informative target locations using scene-adaptive quadrants and warp-based coverage scores, while our Joint Panoramic Diffusion uses Gaussian-splatting-based geometric warps to jointly synthesize four pose-grounded and cross-view-consistent panoramas. A diagnostic-guided global--local attention schedule retains global attention only in sensitive layers, reducing the training and inference costs of high-resolution joint generation. The resulting posed panoramas can be directly fed into an existing reconstruction pipeline to build an explicit 3D representation. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

### 18. [Guidance as Variance Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, introduce variance-matched guidance, picks the guidance scale, guidance DOES NOT improve, guidance each model makes, support guidance can WORK
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801v1>

**原始摘要**

Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model's training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior's covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

### 19. [Generation as Reverse Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34782v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reverse encoding., PATH the semantic encoding, appearance and spatial, semantic encoding PATH, phase revflow-xl achieves FID, space enables few-step generation.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34782v1>

**原始摘要**

Pretrained visual representations accelerate generative learning by abstracting away appearance and spatial detail. Yet the same abstraction makes the return to pixels far harder, shifting much of the burden onto a larger decoder. We ask why the representation-to-pixel path should be learned independently when the encoder already provides a learned pixel-to-semantics path (the semantic encoding path). We introduce RevFlow, based on the principle of generation as reverse encoding. RevFlow uses intermediate representations of a frozen encoder as successive generative targets, first generating deep semantic representations, then shallow representations that retain appearance and spatial detail, before decoding to pixels. To learn these transitions without training a separate generator for each stage, we develop HyC-DiT, a unified generative model with a standard DiT backbone shared across stages and stage-specific condition injection. We further show that native matching in semantic representation space enables few-step generation. RevFlow accelerates both learning and sampling. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, RevFlow reaches 1.96 FID at 690M and 1.74 FID at 1.55B after only 5 training epochs. It also achieves 30.25 dB reconstruction PSNR with an 89M decoder. After a short native-matching adaptation phase, RevFlow-XL achieves 2.85 FID using a single sampling step in each stage. Our code repository is at https://github.com/ChengShiest/RevFlow

### 20. [Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：navigation world, world models, ANY action-conditioned world, NWM and FIND three, WAS the model given, prepared the SAME WAY
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739v1>

**原始摘要**

World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

### 21. [WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：video generation, streaming video, world models, multi-material interactions. video world, generation for multi-material interactions., worldinteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730v1>

**原始摘要**

Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

### 22. [LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction with Limited Real Data: An Architecture Benchmark under Fixed-Epoch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：lidar intensity, intensity reconstruction, benchmark, diffusion, unet-v2 leads with REAL, REAL frames. with real-only
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724v1>

**原始摘要**

Physics-derived simulation can supplement scarce real-world LiDAR intensity measurements, but its effectiveness depends on the learning architecture and the amount of real training data available. We benchmark nine neural architectures for LiDAR intensity reconstruction from depth, semantic labels, incidence angle, and material reflectance, using real SemanticKITTI and synthetic VoxelScape range-image data. All models follow a fixed 20-epoch protocol; this controls the number of training passes, not the computational cost across architectures. We report mean absolute error (MAE), mean squared error (MSE), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity (SSIM), inference timing, and spectral-energy agreement. We also evaluate four selected architectures with fixed-size training mixtures containing 0\%, 1\%, 5\%, 10\%, 20\%, 50\%, or 100\% real frames. With real-only training, UNet-V2 achieves the best reported reconstruction metrics (MAE 0.234, MSE 0.140, PSNR 8.64 dB, and SSIM 0.647), followed by a denoising diffusion model (DDPM). In several mixtures containing 1--10\% real data, DDPM achieves lower error and higher image-quality metrics than the evaluated deterministic alternatives, whereas UNet-V2 leads with 100\% real data. Spectral analysis additionally reveals architecture-dependent discrepancies across frequency bands. These findings characterize reconstruction trade-offs under the stated data mixtures and fixed-epoch protocol, without establishing compute-matched superiority or isolating the diffusion objective from backbone differences.

### 23. [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-grained speculative streaming, world models, roles for fine-grained commitment, fine-grained commitment and advance, complementary roles for fine-grained, long-horizon consistency. shownow turns
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693v1>

**原始摘要**

Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: https://github.com/showlab/ShowNow.

### 24. [PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34690v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：poetry-to-video generation, benchmark, multimodal, text-to-video, assets and shot-level visual, imagery and other non-literal
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34690v1>

**原始摘要**

Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.

### 25. [Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：generative video, neural rendering compiler, benchmark, text-to-video, video generation, video models are becoming
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. NeurCo improves requirement satisfaction from 46.8% to 52.3% on Wan and from 66.5% to 69.3% on Minimax H3, and achieves further gains when combined with prompt enhancers, including on Seedance 2.5. Our analysis reveals model-specific differences in control capabilities and trade-offs between controllability and photorealism, highlighting capability-guided compilation as a promising approach to programmable generative video rendering.

### 26. [Autonomous Ergodic Robotic Palpation for Elastic Mapping of Curved Soft Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34670v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：curved SOFT, robotic palpation, diffusion, geometry-consistent mapping and stiff, properties demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping, SOFT surfaces using point
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34670v1>

**原始摘要**

Autonomous robotic palpation offers repeatable, quantitative estimates of tissue properties for stiff inclusion localization. However, existing methods often rely on prior mesh models or planar surface assumptions that do not generalize to complex human anatomy. We propose an ergodic palpation framework that operates directly on curved soft surfaces using point cloud data. Central to our approach is the Laplace--Beltrami operator, which grounds both online estimation and exploration in the surface intrinsic geometry. We estimate local viscoelastic parameters using an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) and fuse them into a surface-aware Gaussian Process (GP). This probabilistic model drives a diffusion-based ergodic motion planner, generating a continuous exploration trajectory that feeds the new informative samples back to the GP in a closed loop. Throughout this process, a hybrid force-impedance controller regulates interaction forces to maintain contact. We evaluate the approach against existing methods in simulation and validate it in physical experiments on tissue-mimicking silicone phantoms with varying geometries and viscoelastic properties, demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping and stiff inclusion localization under contact-rich conditions.

### 27. [FeaXDrive2: Body-Scene Spatial Grounding for Feasibility-Aware Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34658v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：body-scene spatial, feaxdrive2, spatial grounding., alignment, fine-tuning, feasibility VESF through body-scene
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34658v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative driving planners may produce trajectories that appear feasible at their reference points but violate environmental constraints over the full vehicle body. We propose FeaXDrive2, an end-to-end generative planning framework that enhances vehicle-embodied spatial feasibility (VESF) through body-scene spatial grounding. The proposed method constructs spatial queries from actual vehicle-body corners and dynamically updates visual sampling locations using evolving trajectory estimates across Flow steps. Retrieved local visual features condition subsequent Action Flow-DiT velocity predictions, allowing trajectory generation to guide spatial information retrieval. Rollout-Consistent Safety Fine-Tuning (RC-F) combines high-risk trajectory supervision with reference behavior preservation, followed by planning-supervised VLM-Action Alignment. Vehicle-geometry counterfactual analysis reveals safety differences under identical trajectories but varying vehicle geometry. On NAVSIM, FeaXDrive2 reduces at-fault collisions by 36.5% relative to the base planner and improves PDMS from 87.0 to 88.8. Under NAVSIM v2 evaluation, EPDMS increases from 86.5 to 88.3. Ablation and body-clearance analyses further demonstrate the safety benefits of body-scene spatial grounding.

### 28. [Counting Beyond Frames: Codec Motion Tells Frozen Diffusion VLMs How Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, autoregressive, vision-language, HOW many. counting HOW, counting HOW many times, LIES between the frames
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657v1>

**原始摘要**

Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model's own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

### 29. [Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, readable world models., reasoning, decision-making BUT learning effective, sequential decision-making BUT learning, interaction experience. model-based reinforcement
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

### 30. [Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：visual representations, future visual, object-centric dynamics., object FLOW, attention, diffusion
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644v1>

**原始摘要**

Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

### 31. [Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：POSE control, video generation, beyond POSE, transformer, large-scale data. ultrasound poses, enough and pose-conditioned ultrasound
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622v1>

**原始摘要**

Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

## 高效训练与推理

### 1. [Tracking Any Point with Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34943v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, reasoning, fine-tuning, vision-language, predictions. with gpt-6 astra, point identity and refine
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34943v1>

**原始摘要**

A general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) has surpassed expert vision models in many domains such as object detection and video recognition. However, point correspondence is known to be challenging for VLMs since it goes beyond object-level recognition: it requires pixel-level localization of the same physical surface point across observations, even when appearance changes or the point disappears and later reappears. In this paper, we introduce AgenTAP, a VLM-based agent that directly predicts point coordinates and visibility and actively reasons over videos. Starting from its own estimate, the agent autonomously chooses what to inspect and uses the resulting evidence to verify point identity and refine its predictions. With GPT-6 Astra, it surpasses expert vision models in both point localization and visibility prediction, without correspondence-specific fine-tuning. We further use AgenTAP to re-identify physical points after disappearance and reconnect fragmented tracks, constructing EgoTAP-4D, a dataset of 3D point trajectories in long real-world egocentric videos. On sampled reappearing points, its predictions approach human agreement, and adding EgoTAP-4D to D4RT training improves average 3D tracking performance.

### 2. [Spectrum Adaptation of AI-Native Autoencoder Communications with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34942v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fading with practical front-end, ai-native communications with AES, physical-layer learning replaces separate, end-to-end physical-layer learning replaces, learning. end-to-end physical-layer learning, transfer learning. end-to-end physical-layer
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34942v1>

**原始摘要**

End-to-end physical-layer learning replaces separate modulation, coding, and detection operations with an autoencoder (AE) that consists of a jointly trained encoder-decoder pair at the transmitter and the receiver. This paper studies AI-native communications with AEs and quantifies the sensitivity to training-test mismatch in channel models. Models trained only on an AWGN channel achieve low bit error rates (BERs) under matched conditions but degrade severely on Rayleigh fading with practical front-end impairments. To reduce the time and energy required to adapt to new channel conditions, we propose a low-latency transfer-learning procedure that starts from an AWGN-pretrained system and fine-tunes only a small subset of transmitter and receiver layers while freezing the remainder. We further formulate layer selection as a budget-constrained optimization problem that identifies the most informative parameters to update during adaptation. This preserves the learned signal geometry and the deep decision structure while adapting lightweight front ends to fading- and impairment-induced mixing. For AE communications, we show that partial updates remove the mismatch-induced error floor and approach the BER of fully retrained models. We provide a layer-wise study and quantify resource-performance trade-offs, showing that modest adaptation substantially reduces BER while cutting training time, multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations, energy consumption, and the fraction of updated parameters for resource-efficient implementation.

### 3. [Improving and Expanding Video Editing Capabilities Without Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34941v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：video editor, video editing, editing capabilities, image editing, i2vt improves existing editing, BOTH openve-bench and openve++.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34941v1>

**原始摘要**

Image editing models can follow a wide range of instructions, but video editing models still lag behind, limited in part by the difficulty of collecting paired source and edited videos. Is it possible to transfer advanced image editing capabilities to video editors without new paired videos? We introduce Image-to-Video Transfer (I2VT), which improves pretrained video editors and expands them to new editing tasks using an image editor. It enables efficient post-training with no architectural changes and no new videos, using only individual images. Our key idea is to let the video editor role-play as an image editor: it edits an image as a one-frame video, with the image editor guiding the video editor's predictions at each denoising step. Although the video editor sees only single images during training, at test time it applies the improved and expanded capabilities to whole video clips while preserving temporal consistency. To push the boundaries of video editing, we introduce OpenVE++, an extension of OpenVE-Bench that adds four new editing tasks useful for video production, including action changes, expression changes, body movements, and scene relighting. Our experiments across three different video editors show that I2VT improves existing editing capabilities and expands them to new tasks, and our models achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on both OpenVE-Bench and OpenVE++.

### 4. [LuxAvatar: Real-Time Relightable Head Avatars via Causal Neural Shading of 3D Intrinsic Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal autoregressive neural, neural shader, diffusion, fine-tuning, distillation, framework for real-time HEAD
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940v1>

**原始摘要**

We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers. Conditioned on these buffers, our neural shader formulates forward rendering as a conditional video diffusion process, bypassing explicit PBR computation to synthesize realistic shading effects under target illumination. We achieve real-time streaming through heterogeneous distillation from a pretrained multi-step bidirectional relighting teacher to a two-step causal autoregressive neural shader, inheriting broad synthetic lighting priors while minimizing conditioning and decoding latency. To further specialize these priors to human heads, we construct a hybrid relighting dataset for domain-specific fine-tuning, capturing authentic facial dynamics and physically grounded light transport. Extensive experiments show that our framework enables 3D-controllable, real-time portrait animation and relighting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity, motion control, and lighting accuracy.

### 5. [A Mesh for Every Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：MESH for every, benchmark, dynamic gaussian splatting methods, every frame. existing dynamic, keeps every gaussian, every moment
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939v1>

**原始摘要**

We present MEMO (a Mesh for Every MOment), a method that reconstructs the geometry of complete dynamic scenes, captured by multiple synchronized cameras, and extracts a mesh at every frame. Existing dynamic Gaussian splatting methods either model appearance alone or target isolated objects segmented by foreground masks, and all of them recover meshes by TSDF fusion or Poisson reconstruction, whose fixed grids limit the detail they can capture. At the core of MEMO is a per-Gaussian motion model inspired by Gaussian processes, which represents each Gaussian's position and rotation over time as a combination of temporal Gaussian kernels. The model keeps every Gaussian a valid surface element throughout the sequence and carries an implicit representation of the dynamic geometry, so a mesh can be derived at any time from a field defined by the Gaussians. Although common in real captures, large and fast motion poses a challenge that dynamic Gaussian splatting methods usually overlook. To account for it, we design a track-based initialization scheme that fits each Gaussian's initial trajectory to point tracks in closed form. We evaluate MEMO on Blender-Moments, a new benchmark of nine animated scenes with ground-truth meshes at every frame, and on two real-world datasets. MEMO reconstructs the most accurate geometry among state-of-the-art methods, and remains competitive in novel-view synthesis and training time. Project page: https://memo-4d.github.io

### 6. [Tokenisation is not in APX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34938v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：bottom-up tokenisation under BOTH, direct and bottom-up tokenisation, optimum meaning that tokenisation, unary alphabets direct tokenisation, APX complexity class., apx-hard and THUS admits
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34938v1>

**原始摘要**

In this paper, we prove that, unless P=NP, no polynomial-time algorithm can find a tokeniser whose objective is within any constant factor of the optimum, meaning that tokenisation is not in the APX complexity class. We prove this for direct and bottom-up tokenisation, under both a compression or unigram log-likelihood objectives, and for binary or larger alphabets. We further show that, even over unary alphabets, direct tokenisation is APX-hard, and thus admits no polynomial-time approximation scheme.

### 7. [DexLink: Synthesizing Dexterous Behaviors from Chained Hand-object Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34936v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：synthesizing diverse dexterous, reinforcement learning, dexterous behaviors, control OVER the hand-object, behaviors from chained hand-object, OVER the hand-object interactions.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34936v1>

**原始摘要**

Synthesizing diverse dexterous manipulation policies is challenging: existing simulation-based approaches rely on human demonstrations which are difficult to scale, or use generic reward functions, which provide limited control over the hand-object interactions. We propose DexLink, which addresses these limitations by synthesizing diverse dexterous behaviors from sparse, reusable hand-object interaction templates. Given key task waypoints, DexLink retargets static interaction templates to target objects using an optimization that preserves spatial hand-object relationships. It then links the retargeted poses into an approximate reference trajectory and uses residual reinforcement learning to refine this reference into a closed-loop policy in simulation. Experiments spanning single-hand, bimanual, articulated, and dynamic tasks demonstrate that DexLink provides fine-grained control over task timing, grasp types, and high-level execution. The modular interaction templates transfer reliably across object scales, instances, and categories; without object-specific human demonstrations, the framework also matches the performance of dense reference-tracking methods. Finally, zero-shot deployment of policies trained purely in simulation shows promise for the simulation-based framework.

### 8. [MERLoc: Robust Robot Localization in Dynamic Environments with Pose-Based Retrieval over 3D Gaussian Splatting References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34932v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pose-based retrieval, gaussian splatting, REAL dynamic scenes pose-based, dynamic environments with pose-based, space for the robot, views are sampled OVER
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34932v1>

**原始摘要**

Robots operating in previously mapped spaces must localize accurately despite moving people and rearranged objects. Most localization pipelines match the query against a sparse set of captured reference images retrieved by appearance, which is brittle under scene change and repeating structures. We present MERLoc, which turns a single metric 3D Gaussian Splatting scan into a dense Functional Reference Memory. Reference views are sampled over the navigable space for the robot's morphology and sensors at a target density and rendered offline. Pre-encoding them for the chosen off-the-shelf localizer removes the reference encoder from inference. A pose-based retrieval strategy then selects references by spatial proximity and viewing direction rather than appearance, sidestepping visual ambiguities whenever a prior exists. Across one classical and four learned localizers in simulated and real dynamic scenes, pose-based retrieval matches or outperforms appearance retrieval in most cases, even when the latter is gated by the same prior. On real data, it lowers position RMSE by 15-60 % in nine of ten backend/split combinations, and the rendered memory outperforms real-image caches posed by Structure-from-Motion. The system runs onboard a Jetson Orin NX at 5 Hz, enabling a navigation policy to successfully traverse a hallway with people roaming and rearranging objects.

### 9. [A gene informed benchmark for BioScoring of pathology foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34930v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：pathology foundation, benchmark, foundation models, natural-image models. every pathology, CELL identities, tiers among the pathology
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34930v1>

**原始摘要**

Pathology foundation models are usually compared by their performance on downstream tasks such as classification and retrieval. While such benchmarks measure how useful a model is, they provide no insight into the biology it represents. We introduce BioScore, a benchmark that quantifies how well an image encoder captures cell identities, with spatial transcriptomics of the same tissue as the reference. A sparse autoencoder splits the encoder's patch embeddings into sparse features, which we cluster into a fixed number of groups. BioScore then measures, for each of 52 cell identities defined by marker genes, how specifically one group follows the identity's expression over the tissue. Because the autoencoder training is fully unsupervised, a match shows that the model represents the cell identity from the image alone. We score 8 pathology foundation models and 3 natural-image models. Every pathology model scores above every natural-image model (0.299--0.371 \vs 0.214--0.232), and paired tests separate three tiers among the pathology models. Unlike a supervised probe, BioScore needs no labels, builds its reference from the data and scores even from few sections, yet it ranks the encoders as a probe does. Its per-identity breakdown indicates which cell identities each encoder captures or misses.

### 10. [High-Fidelity Image Matting with Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：high-fidelity image, pixel-space diffusion, image matting, fine-tuning, text-to-image, mismatch with the high-frequency
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929v1>

**原始摘要**

Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone's native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. With pixel-accurate synthetic supervision and real-image fine-tuning, PixelMatte predicts alpha mattes in a single forward pass. Two lightweight calibration modules, LayerShift and High-Pass Refiner, further improve regional opacity accuracy and boundary fidelity. Experiments across four benchmarks and two distinct pixel diffusion backbones demonstrate substantial improvements over existing methods, reducing SAD and gradient errors by 24-44% relative to RenderMatte on P3M-500-NP and AM-2K, while achieving competitive performance on AIM-500 and RenderMatte-2K. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: https://white-yc.github.io/pixelmatte/.

### 11. [Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34928v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diverse alternative reasoning traces, HARD MATH, large language models, deepmath-103k problems DART recovers, greedy-unsolved deepmath-103k problems DART, MATH problems. post-training
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34928v1>

**原始摘要**

Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.

### 12. [One Ocean, Many Tasks: A Data-Grounded Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Marine Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34925v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：data-grounded simulation suite, simulation suite and benchmark, agent, data-driven currents and tides, disturbances and reveal trade-offs, navigation and station-keeping under
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34925v1>

**原始摘要**

Simulation is essential for marine robotics, yet existing platforms often trade off between visually realistic worlds, high-throughput experimentation, and ocean-condition realism grounded in real data. We present OneOcean, a data-grounded simulation suite and benchmark built around a unified spatiotemporal ocean-environment product that harmonizes bathymetry with data-driven currents and tides, with optional pollution fields for cleanup-related evaluation. On top of this environment representation, OneOcean defines a task ladder spanning navigation and station-keeping under currents, waypoint and route following, depth-profile tracking, area scanning, pipeline inspection, and multi-agent coordination, including formation transit, fish protection and patrol (herding), and surface pollution localization, containment, and cleanup. The suite supports multi-agent settings with 2-10 vehicles. Our simulator supports three to six Degrees of Freedom (DoF) and produces standardized metrics and reproducible run manifests, enabling consistent aggregation across tasks, difficulties, and scaling settings. Experiments across dataset variants and scenes systematically stress planning and control under data-grounded disturbances and reveal trade-offs among heuristic baselines, Behavior Cloning (BC), and a Large Language Model (LLM)-based planner.

### 13. [Mixture-of-Loops Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：transformer, MoE, attention, mixture-of-loops MOL decoding beyond, unlocks single-traversal mixture-of-loops MOL, single-traversal mixture-of-loops MOL decoding
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923v1>

**原始摘要**

Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Pushing their expressivity-efficiency frontier requires rethinking the core mechanism connecting adjacent loops: What should be passed as a Transformer loops? We introduce Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT), whose Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) uses keys and values (KV) as memory for selective reuse across depths and loops. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel training, unlocks single-traversal Mixture-of-Loops (MoL) decoding beyond the training loop horizon. To balance efficiency and quality, we introduce U-shaped MoLT (U-MoLT), which separates memory construction from prediction readout and skips unused decoder traversals without changing predictions. In 400B-token pretraining, U-MoLT outperforms OLMo3 across 700M/1.5B dense and 3B MoE models, achieving 1.7x validation cross-entropy (CE) token efficiency at 700M while improving 3B MoE 10-domain perplexity by 0.75 and 10-task accuracy by 2.1 percentage points. In 100B-token continued pretraining of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, our U-MoLT adaptation lowers CE on all 11 domains over the matched baseline with 1.4x mean token efficiency. At inference, U-MoLT with MoL-Hybrid-2 matches non-looped OLMo3 latency at 996.6 vs. 1,000.6 ms on S-size (~113M) models. Code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT

### 14. [$μ$-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-User Task Completion in Shared Interaction Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34920v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, joint task success, benchmark, multi-user requests while upholding, multi-user simulation with verifiable, session-level outcomes bench provides
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34920v1>

**原始摘要**

Voice agents in shared spaces must satisfy interdependent multi-user requests while upholding per-user authority, privacy, and task constraints. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage when participants react throughout a session. We introduce $μ$-Bench, a benchmark for joint task completion in closed-loop, full-duplex multi-user voice interaction. Simulated participants act from isolated profiles of goals, knowledge, and permissions, adapting to the agent's speech and tool outcomes. Across 20 domains, 100 scenarios vary group size, acoustics, and follow-up demands to form 800 cases (408 tool-augmented, 392 tool-free). Joint Task Success (JTS) requires all user goals, privacy boundaries, and terminal states to hold jointly, while Pure JTS further disallows unauthorized tool calls; Attribution, Threading, and Coordination (ATC) diagnostics localize failures. Across 13 voice agents, proprietary systems peak at 62.24\% tool-free and 65.44\% tool-augmented success, while open-weight models remain below 20\%. ATC diagnostics show that open-weight models lag primarily in multi-turn attribution and threading, and controlled contrasts reveal that larger groups consistently degrade joint task success across capable systems. By coupling reactive multi-user simulation with verifiable session-level outcomes, $μ$-Bench provides a rigorous testbed for shared voice agents.

### 15. [Memory Is Not Ground Truth: Revising Navigation Authority under Unreliable VLM Evidence for Persistent Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34915v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reasoning, vision-language, remain ambiguous under vlm-based, about semantic and spatio-temporal, semantic and spatio-temporal context, ambiguous under vlm-based perception.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34915v1>

**原始摘要**

Vision-language models (VLMs) enable robots to reason about semantic and spatio-temporal context, verify task-relevant objects, and guide sensing and navigation. Persistent memory can reduce redundant search for field robots, but when should a memory continue to guide behavior? Repeated misses may suggest that a memory is stale. Our experiments show why this evidence can remain ambiguous under VLM-based perception.
