Using a Boston Dynamics Spot with four fixed cameras and a persistent spatio-temporal visual memory, we measure negative-verification behavior across targets, viewpoints, language queries, and repeated revisits. False-negative rates vary substantially across objects, nearby revisits often reproduce the same detector outcome, and some matched target-present and target-removed experiments yield identical miss histories. This ambiguity persists under changed observation geometry, identity-preserving query reformulation, and conditioning on a previously verified target image. These findings raise two questions: is the object still there, and is this memory still useful for navigation? We introduce a dependence-aware, reversible memory-update mechanism that avoids double-counting correlated misses and stops using unsupported memories as direct navigation goals without claiming that the object is absent. Later positive evidence can restore a memory's use for navigation. One resident onboard VLM is shared across memory verification, semantic frontier reasoning, and in-motion perception. In three matched real-robot decision contexts, an episode-local baseline selected a stale memory as its navigation objective in every case, whereas our method selected semantic frontiers in all three. A complementary trial re-verified the target during motion and promoted fresh memory. These results support revising memory actionability rather than interpreting failed detection as object absence.

### 16. [Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, fine-tuning, vision-language, VLAS are capable low-level, keeps the VLA frozen, VLA frozen and steers
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34914v1>

**原始摘要**

Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA's intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA's denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object's poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA's weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen π0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a π0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID π0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm's reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

### 17. [LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal window attention, video VAE, diffusion, distillation, minimax-h3 livevae achieves, THAN the official VAE
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912v1>

**原始摘要**

While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

### 18. [Agentic LongLive: Asynchronous Agentic Memory for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agentic longlive, memory for LONG video, agentic memory for LONG, LONG video generation., asynchronous agentic memory, reinforcement learning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907v1>

**原始摘要**

Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. From this entity memory it composes a global image and a per-shot memory image, which an auxiliary GPU prefills into the generator's attention sink before the next shot starts, and writes entity cards for the prompts that name them, so the video never waits for memory and video errors are not written back. A small language model, trained with reinforcement learning and an image reward calibrated using downstream video outcomes, decides what to remember before the next prompt is known. On LongStory-Online, a new benchmark of 25 stories (857 shots) revealed one shot at a time, Agentic LongLive improves persistent-state accuracy of LongLive-2.0, its base generator, by 26.3 points, protagonist identity by 44.9, and revisited-place consistency by 40.4, at about 1% extra generation time on H100. The full system streams faster than real time on one 8xB200 node with the generator in BF16 (34.3 FPS), keeps image quality stable over 14-minute stories, and improves identity on two MiniMax-H3 generators, one of which, LongLive-H3, also runs in real time with it. To our knowledge, it is the first real-time video generator with text and image memory kept by an LLM agent.

### 19. [ProbFaS: Probabilistic Video Frame Selection for MLLMs via Multi-Fidelity Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34903v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：MLVU and longvideobench probfas, frame selection, alignment, multimodal, large language models, unifying THEM via co-kriging
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34903v1>

**原始摘要**

Processing long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is prohibitively expensive, making informative frame selection essential for accurate video understanding. The default uniform sampling often misses task-relevant keyframes, while recent training-free selectors instead pick frames by how well each one matches the question under an image-text alignment (ITA) score. However, we show that ITA signals correspond poorly with the MLLM's true needs: their rankings correlate weakly with downstream accuracy and are even negatively correlated on over 40% of questions, whereas the MLLM's internal yes/no judgment yields a much stronger signal. We present ProbFaS, a training- and label-free probabilistic pipeline that fuses both signals for key frame selection. ProbFaS treats dense ITA scores as cheap, biased measurements and sparse MLLM judgments as accurate, costly ones, unifying them via co-kriging, a multi-fidelity Gaussian process, to calibrate relevance across all frames. A temporal determinantal point process then selects frames capturing relevance peaks while preserving temporal coverage. Across Video-MME, MLVU and LongVideoBench, ProbFaS is the most accurate among fourteen state-of-the-art training-free selectors and uniform sampling in 13 of 15 settings (8-128 frames), outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 3.1 percentage points. On MLVU and LongVideoBench, 32 ProbFaS frames match or surpass the top baseline at 128 frames and uniform sampling with up to 768 frames.

### 20. [Enhancing Maritime Domain Awareness using Latent Space Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34899v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：maritime domain awareness, latent space, benchmark, foundation models, sentinel-1, latent-space representations rather THAN
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34899v1>

**原始摘要**

This work presents a novel application of Earth Observation (EO) embeddings for Maritime Domain Awareness (MDA) from the perspective of the geospatial foundation model TerraMind. By operating directly on compressed latent-space representations rather than on the original Sentinel-1 imagery, this approach directly addresses the data gravity bottleneck that increasingly constrains operational EO processing, in line with the objectives of the Embed2Scale project. In particular, linear and non-linear probing of compressed embeddings, latent space representations of synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery, is used to detect, localise, and characterise vessels from Sentinel-1 GRD images. Vessel detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.95, while vessel localisation has a median error of around 300 m for vessels longer than 100 m, and vessel AIS type-code classification achieves 62% accuracy for the majority classes. The latter, however, is highly correlated with vessel length. These results reveal both the potential and current limitations of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) when applied to fine-grained MDA understanding in real-world scenarios. Additionally, a new maritime remote sensing dataset is released, pairing about 2,000 Sentinel-1 images with temporally aligned AIS signals from commercial vessels. Through manual inspection, each candidate sample was verified for bounding-box quality and for consistency between the AIS-reported vessel length and the observed vessel extent, cross-checking vessel identity and location against publicly available vessel records and, where available, photographs. The dataset was made ready for use with the NeuCo-Bench neural compression benchmark framework, publicly available on HuggingFace through https://huggingface.co/datasets/embed2scale/S1-single-ship-downstream .

### 21. [The Look-Elsewhere Effect in Searches for New Physics: from Classical Methods to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34888v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：searches for new physics, look-elsewhere effect, BOTH classical and ml-based, classical and ml-based searches, machine learning. the look-elsewhere, excess and the local
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34888v1>

**原始摘要**

The look-elsewhere effect (LEE) arises when the location of an effect in parameter space is not specified in advance: scanning many locations inflates the significance of the largest excess, and the local $p$-value must be corrected into a global one. A well-established framework, rooted in the work of Davies and operationalised for particle physics by Gross and Vitells, provides this correction for scans over continuous parameters. Some aspects, however, remain unsettled. The definition of "elsewhere" is ambiguous and ranges from a single distribution to an entire experimental programme. Moreover, the analytical shortcuts of the classical framework do not carry over to searches based on machine learning (ML), which are affected by an implicit post-selection bias that depends on the model's resolution and expressiveness. This review covers the LEE in searches for new physics at colliders, from its statistical foundations to these modern challenges. We discuss the corrections for continuous scans and for multiple discrete tests, illustrated with simple numerical examples, and the interaction between ML and the LEE, which we connect to the broader literature on selective inference. We highlight open problems, including the computational cost of empirical calibration for complex models and the development of analytical shortcuts for ML test statistics, and offer recommendations for reporting discovery significance in both classical and ML-based searches in particle physics.

### 22. [Interpretable and Accurate Hybrid Decision Trees with Selective Case-Based Splits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34887v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：decision trees, selective case-based, axis-parallel and oblique splits, BOTH feature-based and case-based, feature-based and case-based splits, proximity-based splits using exemplar
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34887v1>

**原始摘要**

Decision trees are among the most well-known interpretable models in Machine Learning. Over the years, a wide variety of strategies have been proposed to define split conditions within the greedy learning framework, aiming to guide instance routing and enhance predictive performance. These strategies range from axis-parallel and oblique splits based on feature values to proximity-based splits using exemplar instances and more complex hybrid approaches. However, no single method has emerged as universally superior. In this work, we present two key contributions to the construction of greedy decision trees. First, we propose a hybrid tree architecture that integrates both feature-based and case-based splits, allowing for heterogeneous splits throughout the tree. Second, we introduce a novel type of split that leverages a selective case-based approach: when a split is needed, our method searches for the most informative subset of features to define the split, effectively balancing the specificity of case-based methods with the simplicity of feature-based ones. Experiments on a diverse set of tabular datasets show that our method outperforms standard decision trees and achieves performance on par with ensemble methods, while producing shallow trees that retain or even improve predictive accuracy.

### 23. [Stranger Things: When Objects Appear Without Their Typical Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34881v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：WHEN objects appear, benchmark, multimodal, transformer, vision-language, large language models
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34881v1>

**原始摘要**

Visual context is a powerful cue for object recognition: scenes inform what, where, and at what scale objects are likely to appear. Yet over-reliance on context becomes a failure mode when models predict objects based on their typical neighbors rather than the objects' own visual features, which we call typicality bias. While extensively studied in the pre-Transformer era, it has often been assumed that architectural and scale improvements would naturally solve it, and a systematic diagnostic across the modern vision stack remains absent. We provide such a diagnostic framework. We measure typicality bias via normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI) over object pairs, and introduce STRANGE-Bench (Synthetic Typicality-RANked Grounded Evaluation Benchmark): a photorealistic, layout-controlled benchmark spanning 17 sub-tiers from typical to never-co-occurring object pairs. Across five model families (specialist detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, self-supervised, vision-language, and multimodal large language models) and three recognition tasks (supervised detection, open-vocabulary detection, and classification), the bias is prevalent: gaps of 5-11 p.p. persist when objects appear outside their typical company, and do not consistently shrink with scale. To mitigate it, we turn to data augmentation: existing augmentations are designed for visual diversity, not for breaking co-occurrence shortcuts, and sample from the very distribution that produces typicality bias. We propose Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS), a simple sampling rule for paste-based augmentations that uses the NPMI signal to tilt the training-time co-occurrence distribution toward atypical partners. AAS reduces the typicality gap by up to 1.6 p.p. on natural-image COCO splits and 23.0 p.p. on synthetic STRANGE-Bench across three model families, without sacrificing overall performance.

### 24. [Towards Closing the Performance Gap in Cross-Modal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：place recognition, cross-modal place, attention, fine-tuning, text-to-image, vision-language
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877v1>

**原始摘要**

Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. Stage 1 performs global retrieval by pairing a jointly aligned DINOv2-B/14 visual backbone with a progressively fine-tuned BGE-Large text encoder, while Stage 2 refines top candidates using a lightweight token-level cross-attention reranker. Through systematic analysis, we isolate the drivers of retrieval accuracy and show that visual token density provides the largest performance improvement, while text encoder capacity and task fine-tuning offer complementary gains. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. Code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/oferidan1/CrossPlace

### 25. [How Much Do Coding Agents Over-Edit, and Why? An Empirical Study of Unneeded Edits in Repository-Level Agent Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34875v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：swe-bench verified, agent, extra edits, unneeded CODE, RATE and that over-editing, THAN developers
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34875v1>

**原始摘要**

Agent patches are larger than developers', which is widely read as over-editing. But patch size does not say whether the extra edits are needed, what they are, or where they come from. We introduce Delta Residue Attribution (DRA), which replays agent trajectories, traces every block (unit) of the final patch to the step that wrote it, reverts each unit, and reruns the task tests to decide whether the unit is needed. We apply DRA to 7,660 resolved GitHub Copilot CLI trajectories from 18 frontier models on SWE-bench Verified (6,728) and DeepSWE (932), and grade developer patches using the same method. Agent patches do change more lines than developers'; on SWE-bench Verified the majority of extra edits add tests (61%) and documentation (13%) while on DeepSWE, most extra edits are code (58%). On both benchmarks, agents' code is on average at least as necessary as developers' (mean $NP_{code}$ 0.887 vs. 0.864 on SWE-bench Verified, where 11 of 18 models leave less unneeded code than developers, and 0.797 vs. 0.785 on DeepSWE). On average, 11% of an agent patch's code units on SWE-bench Verified are still unneeded, and 89% of those change behavior. Debug steps write almost none of the unneeded code. Code necessity differs substantially by model; it is unrelated to resolve rate on SWE-bench Verified and negatively correlated with it on DeepSWE (Spearman -0.59, p = 0.01). On SWE-bench Verified, pruning with DRA using only the tests the agent can see (its own tests and the repository's) removes the majority of unneeded code but also some needed code (67% recall, 46% precision), and 89% of pruned patches still resolve the task; asking the agent to audit its own diff is more precise but less effective (36% recall, 66% precision). We recommend that benchmarks report code necessity next to resolve rate and that over-editing be addressed through model training and harness-level pruning.

### 26. [Shape-Aware Three-Way Merge for RDF Knowledge Graphs: The schema defines the conflict: an implementation and evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34870v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：three-way merge, schema defines, agent, claim the first three-way, seeds the shape-aware operator, synthetic seeds the shape-aware
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34870v1>

**原始摘要**

Merging RDF serializations creates ordering conflicts and misses semantic ones. We describe and evaluate a three-way merge in which the schema defines a conflict: set algebra combines canonical triples, and a slot is contended only where a SHACL shape bounds its cardinality and both sides' changes cannot stand within that bound. Unbounded predicates union; contended slots go to a person. The same shapes graph validates the result. We do not claim the first three-way merge for RDF. Our contribution is an implementation in a production multi-agent store and an evaluation against a reimplementation of Quit Store's Context Merge overlap rule, which it extends rather than replaces, and six other strategies. Across five synthetic seeds, the shape-aware operator raised 33 conflicts, all true positives as judged by the same shapes that define them, with no triples lost or fabricated and no SHACL violations admitted. The context-overlap baseline, counted per slot, detected the same 33 conflicts but asked 845 questions. A replay built from recorded production incidents covers 105 human alias repairs (93 after excluding 12 chained pairs) and 939 duplicate-value incidents. It reproduces a triple-level limit: different names for one entity remain invisible (0 of 93 alias repairs detected, matching 0 of 21 synthetic cases). In a study of evolving shape contracts, merging shape declarations first handles the tested cardinality changes, while datatype evolution exposes a detector blind spot caught by post-merge validation. An oracle that reads no shapes agrees with the shipped operator on the facts and history of all 23 reachable single-slot cases. Under wrong shapes, a missing bound loses data silently and a spurious one only adds questions. The supported claim is fewer human decisions with nothing silently lost, for cardinality conflicts in one deployment, not general merge correctness.

### 27. [A Survey of Security, Privacy, and Ethics of Large Language Models in Telecom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34868v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：privacy security, large language models, cross-border compliance and scalable, longitudinal data privacy cross-border, telecom-specific taxonomy linking privacy, privacy cross-border compliance
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34868v1>

**原始摘要**

Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into telecommunications for customer service, network analytics, and operational support. While offering efficiency and innovation, their deployment raises critical privacy, security, and ethical challenges due to the uniquely sensitive and longitudinal nature of telecom data. This paper presents a survey of LLM risks and safeguards in telecom context. Our survey introduces a telecom-specific taxonomy linking privacy, security, and ethics with use cases and outlines open research challenges such as longitudinal data privacy, cross-border compliance, and scalable guardrails.

### 28. [RePace: Reasoning about Demonstration Speedup for Faster Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34864v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：VLA policies, speedup factor, reasoning, vision-language, alignment, fine-tuning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34864v1>

**原始摘要**

Since vision-language-action (VLA) policies can inherit the slow pace of human demonstrations, accelerating these demonstrations before fine-tuning can lead to faster VLA policies without an additional speed-selection module at deployment. Several offline speedup methods have focused on identifying segments that require less precision and assigning predefined speedup factors to the segments. However, even within a single segment, a speedup factor suitable for steady motion may be too high when precise alignment is required. To this end, this paper presents RePace, an offline reasoning framework that determines how much to accelerate each part of a demonstration. For the same recorded movement, RePace measures how much each candidate speedup factor shortens the sequence and compares the resulting observation-action pairs used for training. Given the task instruction, a pretrained language model uses the visual context and original motion of successive windows to interpret these candidate retiming effects and select a speedup profile for the complete episode. Experimental results demonstrate that RePace outperforms the baselines in overall task execution efficiency on both LIBERO and RoboCasa-Kitchen. On all three tasks with a gripper-equipped Franka Research 3 arm, RePace matches or exceeds the acceleration baselines in success rate and completes successful trials faster.

### 29. [Focus Only on What's Important: Training-Free Focus Maps for Frozen Vision Transformers in Long-Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34863v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：protecting ONLY the previous, training-free focus, vision transformers, object segmentation., frozen dinov3 VIT inside, training-free token merging CUTS
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34863v1>

**原始摘要**

Vision transformers are strong but costly image encoders for video object segmentation. Training-free token merging cuts compute uniformly across the frame, including on tracked objects. We give a frozen DINOv3 ViT inside a trained Cutie head a training-free focus map: full resolution where the segmenter expects its objects and coarser merging elsewhere. Protecting only the previous frame's mask works on short clips but fails on long videos, where fast motion pushes targets outside the margin and into background merging, locking in loss. Our policy instead takes the union of velocity-shifted and block-matched masks, runs a camera-compensated surprise check, delegates uncertain regions to the compared merger, and falls back to it when all objects are lost. On long videos (LVOS V2 valid, pre-specified protocol, three ViT-S seeds), it beats ALGM 0.98 by +2.76 J&F (95% object CI [+1.22,+4.44]; video-cluster [+0.99,+4.52]) at 16% fewer counted encoder FLOPs (0.385 vs. 0.459), sitting 1.0 J&F point below the dense-encoder baseline; at ViT-B the gain over ALGM 0.95 is +3.89 [+2.03,+5.87]. On DAVIS 2017 val it stays within -0.19 J&F of the dense encoder at ViT-S (-0.12 at ViT-B). Controls qualify the result: protecting only the previous mask under ALGM matches or exceeds accuracy at higher FLOPs (0.486), so expectation and surprise mainly reduce counted encoder compute. Practically, lower counted encoder compute did not reduce startup-inclusive mean Cutie step time on a 12-video LVOS sample (3%-8% lower in steady state; an equally engineered ALGM is faster at ViT-S and ViT-B). Section 5.2 separately examines a Cutie permanent-memory schedule and its latency and memory trade-offs. Code: https://github.com/boletoms/focused-cv

### 30. [BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark for monocular, bop-refer the first benchmark, major proprietary and open-weight, proprietary and open-weight families, vlm-generated and human-verified referring, MORE THAN the object
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. We pair images from these datasets with VLM-generated and human-verified referring expressions, and derive accurate ground-truth 3D and 2D bounding boxes of the referred objects from their 3D models and 6DoF pose annotations. Our systematic evaluation of 17 frontier VLMs across the major proprietary and open-weight families (Gemini, GPT, Claude, Qwen, Gemma) shows that the best model, Gemini 4 Carbon, reaches 72.6% AP_IoU2D in 2D but only 12.4% AP_IoU3D in 3D, despite the more lenient IoU thresholds in 3D. The predicted 3D boxes are usually centered on the target objects in the image but often off in depth by more than the object's size, suggesting that metric scale estimation, which needs reliable object size priors, is currently by far the main source of error.

### 31. [DocDUR-Net: Degradation-Aware Unified Document Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34860v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：document image, degradation-aware unified, unified document, image restoration, restoration methods, unified restoration
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34860v1>

**原始摘要**

Captured document images often suffer from diverse degradations caused by uncontrolled imaging conditions, which reduce readability and obscure fine text structures. Single-degradation restoration methods are usually trained and deployed for individual degradation types, and therefore require model selection before restoration. Unified restoration models alleviate the need to maintain multiple restoration networks by sharing one network across tasks, but representative unified document image restoration methods still depend on external task selection. This dependency limits automatic document image restoration. We propose DocDUR-Net, a degradation-aware unified restoration network that derives restoration guidance directly from the input image. It introduces a Degradation Perception Module (DPM) to extract degradation representations and predict degradation weights, and Degradation Prior-guided Adaptive Feature Fusion (DP-AFF) to combine learnable degradation priors with degradation features extracted from the input. The resulting guidance is injected into a shared restoration backbone, enabling adaptive restoration without external task selection. DocDUR-Net also supports a manual mode with explicit task selection. Experiments on document image restoration benchmarks show that DocDUR-Net achieves the best average PSNR of 29.405 dB and SSIM of 0.960 among unified methods while remaining competitive with single-degradation methods.

### 32. [From Prediction to Decision: Decision-Focused Fine-Tuning of Autoregressive Tabular Transformers for Cost-Sensitive Financial Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34858v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, autoregressive, tabular transformers for cost-sensitive, cost-sensitive learning whose per-example, transformers for cost-sensitive financial, example-dependent cost-sensitive learning whose
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34858v1>

**原始摘要**

Autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized tabular event streams are a simple and strong baseline for predicting financial events such as fraud. Yet institutions do not act on probabilities; they act on decisions whose consequences are asymmetric and heteroscedastic: the cost of a missed high-value fraud and that of a wrongly declined small purchase are not the same. The standard bridge is post-hoc Bayes-optimal thresholding of calibrated probabilities under a cost matrix. We ask whether fine-tuning the model to the decision objective can do better. We fine-tune the same pretrained model so that its restricted softmax over label tokens (already a distribution over business actions) maximizes expected utility under an institution-specified, amount-scaled cost matrix. Because the reward is a known function of (action, outcome, amount) and the outcome is observed in the data, the expected-utility objective and its gradient are available in closed form. This is example-dependent cost-sensitive learning whose per-example loss is linear in the predicted probability, with no sampling, critics, or advantage estimation. A KL anchor to the pretrained model and an auxiliary language-modeling term preserve calibration and the generative event model. A sampled, group-normalized (GRPO-style) variant provides the extension point to trajectory-level rewards.

### 33. [An offset--drift Kalman filter for continuous emotion recognition and annotation from sparse human reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34857v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：continuous emotion recognition, offset--drift kalman filter, ratings and the traces, FEW human reviews could, HAND OVER and adaptation, HOW emotion changes OVER
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34857v1>

**原始摘要**

Continuous emotion recognition tracks how emotion changes over time, but its predictions often deviate from human ratings, and the traces it is trained on are rated in real time by several annotators, which is slow and costly. A few human reviews could help with both, but rejection only decides which predictions to hand over and adaptation adjusts the model or its outputs; neither uses reviews to correct unreviewed windows directly. We propose a Kalman-filter correction that models the predictor's error as a per-part offset plus a slow drift, estimates it from the reviewed windows and corrects every unreviewed window online, leaving the predictor unchanged. On MSP-Conversation, it beats four simpler corrections in all 48 paired comparisons, keeps correcting each part after reviewing stops and, for uncorrected predictions, tells a person which windows to take over. The same correction serves annotation: with 20% of windows reviewed, the completed trace comes closer to the raters.

### 34. [TIDE-Flow: Fine-Grained Temporal Evidence and Structured Displacement Matching for Spike Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34854v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-grained temporal, displacement evidence, MAY weaken fine-grained firing, RAW spike patterns multi-scale, process MAY weaken fine-grained, spike cameras capture high-speed
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34854v1>

**原始摘要**

Spike cameras capture high-speed motion through an integrate-and-fire mechanism, providing fine-grained temporal observations without exposure blur. However, their binary streams encode accumulated illumination rather than instantaneous intensity, making reliable motion estimation challenging. Existing spike-based methods exploit temporal aggregation to construct motion representations, but this process may weaken fine-grained firing patterns. Meanwhile, correlation-based estimators often rely on learned matching features without explicitly characterizing the spatial distribution and ambiguity of candidate displacements. To address these challenges, we propose TIDE-Flow, a spike-based optical flow estimation framework that jointly exploits Temporal Integration Evidence (TIE) and Displacement Evidence Encoding (DEE). TIE combines raw spike patterns, multi-scale firing statistics, temporal moments, and boundary-aware interval cues, preserving complementary temporal information before spatial feature compression. DEE further integrates structured candidate-grid features with explicit displacement statistics to characterize matching geometry and competing correspondences. Both components are incorporated into a difference-correlation recurrent refinement framework, enabling effective temporal motion modeling and correspondence estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of TIDE-Flow, with controlled ablations validating the contributions of temporal and displacement evidence modeling.

### 35. [DRBench Pro: A Knowledge-Centric Benchmark for Professional Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34852v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：DEEP research, agent, benchmark, drbench PRO, research directions, research queries and evidence-grounded
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34852v1>

**原始摘要**

Deep research agents gather information from multiple sources and synthesize it into reports on complex, open-ended questions. Existing benchmarks assess report quality along several dimensions, but lack a dedicated knowledge-centric evaluation of which research directions a report covers and how deeply it explores them. We introduce DRBench Pro, a benchmark that makes structured knowledge coverage the primary evaluation target. Our automated pipeline builds local knowledge trees from Wikipedia and uses sampled subgraphs to generate research queries and evidence-grounded rubrics. Each rubric corresponds to a relation in the tree, preserving the structure of the knowledge being evaluated. Agents receive only the research query. This structure allows us to measure breadth across major research directions and depth along related knowledge chains, with their geometric mean giving overall coverage. The benchmark includes 144 cases across 24 domains and 44,102 rubrics. Evaluating 16 deep research agents reveals different strengths in breadth and depth, with the best-performing agent reaching 57.04% overall coverage. Rubric judgments agree closely with human annotations, and agent rankings remain stable across the tested metric configurations.

### 36. [Real2SimLab: A Unified Platform for Real-to-Simulation Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34849v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：real2simlab, unified platform for real-to-simulation, platform for real-to-simulation transfer., benchmark, agent, real-to-simulation papers are OPEN
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34849v1>

**原始摘要**

Real-to-simulation transfer, which builds simulations from real-world observations, has long been studied in research and industry. Recently, coding agents have become capable of generating simulation programs from video recordings, and several benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate them. However, each individual benchmark defines its own inputs, deliverables, and scoring pipeline. This leads to $N \times M$ integrations when evaluating $N$ methods on $M$ benchmarks. To this end, we present Real2SimLab, a unified platform for real-to-simulation transfer. It evaluates a method by running its written program and scoring the output simulation with the original scorer of each benchmark. Moreover, Real2SimLab provides a pipeline to create training data and reward annotations for real-to-simulation methods. Our pipeline carefully avoids data contamination by filtering out the video recordings that exist in benchmarks. For each video recording, a program is first produced, and then our pipeline computes its reward with the same code that Real2SimLab uses for evaluation. So far, it supports 47 coding agents and 5 non-agent methods on 6 benchmarks. Both the platform and Awesome-Real2Sim, our research map of nearly 300 real-to-simulation papers, are open source, and we invite the Real2SimHub community to build them together.

### 37. [Put the World Back: Timestamps Matter in Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34848v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：TIME series, reasoning, alignment, large language models, TIME semantics and event-series, PUT the world BACK
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34848v1>

**原始摘要**

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to time series reasoning. However, current evaluations often give models events in chronological order and already matched to the time series. This makes it unclear whether models use timestamps to connect events with observations or simply take shortcuts. To expose this ambiguity, we reformulate time series reasoning from a world-to-series projection perspective, viewing timestamps as explicit indices that connect numerical observations and external events to shared world states. We identify three structures that timestamps can make explicit: temporal causal structure, time semantics, and event-series alignment. Building on this perspective, we develop a diagnostic protocol that distinguishes timestamp-grounded reasoning from correlational shortcuts, a distinction that accuracy-based benchmarks cannot make. Controlled interventions on 310 questions from two tasks show that high accuracy can persist when timestamps are replaced by relative offsets or removed, while breaking event-series alignment induces systematic failures. When context is pre-aligned, models barely need timestamps. When order is missing, they use dates to recall world knowledge. Without dates, they rely on correlational shortcuts and rarely notice incorrect timestamps. These findings show that high accuracy on pre-aligned, ordered inputs does not establish that a model uses timestamps to synchronize observations with the world. We release our code, model outputs, and trajectory labels at https://github.com/tingrui-huang/timestamps-matter.

### 38. [Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent: Self-Structuring Learning through Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：knowledge-base gradient descent, self-structuring learning, large language models, knowledge BASE, gradient descent self-structuring, EDIT WHEN the learning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841v1>

**原始摘要**

We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

### 39. [Provably Efficient Offline Discounted Reinforcement Learning under Recursive Entropic Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34840v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, under recursive, recursive entropic, BOTH risk-averse and risk-seeking, agent, finite discounted infinite-horizon markov
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34840v1>

**原始摘要**

We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted infinite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with recursive entropic risk measures (ERM), parameterized by $β\neq 0$. We consider both risk-averse ($β>0$) and risk-seeking ($β<0$) settings. We consider the offline RL setting, where the agent is provided with a fixed dataset and wishes to learn a near-optimal policy using this dataset. We introduce a model-based algorithm, called ERM Value Iteration Lower Confidence Bound (ERM-VI-LCB) and derive a PAC-type bound on its sample complexity. We also establish a corresponding lower bound, demonstrating that exponential dependence on $|β|/(1-γ)$ asserted by the upper bound is unavoidable in the worst case. The presented bounds provide the first rigorous sample complexity guarantees for offline RL under recursive ERM across both risk-averse and risk-seeking regimes.

### 40. [Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：alignment, benchmark, diffusion, text-to-image, editing and introduce seam-bench, image editing and introduce
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839v1>

**原始摘要**

Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

### 41. [Repairing the Trustworthiness of Distilled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34831v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：preference optimization, knowledge distillation, large language models, post-training models while largely, smaller student models BUT, BUT the compression process
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34831v1>

**原始摘要**

Knowledge distillation enables the deployment of large language models through smaller student models, but the compression process can introduce regressions in trustworthiness even when downstream performance is preserved. Repairing such failures is challenging because the original distillation corpus is often unavailable, while direct preference optimization may alter capabilities inherited from the teacher. We propose a targeted repair framework based on the proximal Bregman response function (PBRF) that addresses both limitations. We estimate the curvature required for PBRF using a surrogate proxy dataset, removing dependence on the original transfer corpus, and extend the framework to preference-based supervision through an odds-ratio objective. This enables the use of preferred and dispreferred response pairs while constraining changes to the distilled model. Our results show that the proposed approach improves targeted trustworthiness behaviors for both pre-training and post-training models while largely preserving downstream performance, providing a practical alternative to re-distillation or unconstrained preference optimization. Our experiment results show that across different distilled models, the trustworthy regression can be improved by up to 8% with less than 1% decrement in perplexity.

### 42. [Beyond Feature Fusion: Sensor-Grounded Semantic-Spiking Learning for Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34828v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：sensor-grounded semantic-spiking, semantic-spiking learning for robust, beyond feature fusion sensor-grounded, framework for illumination-robust object, semantic-spiking framework for illumination-robust, illumination-robust object detection
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34828v1>

**原始摘要**

Underexposure weakens object details, and overexposure obscures boundaries. Spike cameras encode light into high-frequency binary streams. Long-window spike counts give stable appearance cues, while short observations reveal changes in firing rates from sparse measurements. We propose SPIRE, a sensor-grounded semantic-spiking framework for illumination-robust object detection from a single spike stream. SPIRE builds an appearance view from the full packet through detection-oriented illumination adaptation and constructs a sequence of cumulative views from progressively longer prefixes. Each prefix supplies a rate map, an edge map, and a rate difference that tracks changes as spikes accumulate. At selected scales, ANN features and spike-derived initialization jointly set the initial membrane potentials and firing thresholds, allowing semantic cues to shape the responses during temporal encoding. Local spike-count contrasts supervise both branches during training. The residual between measured and ANN-predicted contrasts combines with the spiking features to produce a bounded correction to the fused feature pyramid. On simulated VOC and COCO, SPIRE-D outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.00-5.50 AP50 points across the four under- and overexposure settings. On real PKU-Vidar-DVS recordings, it achieves 67.07 AP50 and 40.10 AP75. SPIRE-D also uses 50.4% fewer MACs than SPFD.

### 43. [Routeability Before Routing: Routeability Audit Protocol (RAP) and RouteabilityBench for Audited LLM Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34825v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：RAP and routeabilitybench, benchmark, GPQA mmlu-pro and ifeval, claim-verification package. the audit, details and family-corrected statistical, construction details and family-corrected
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34825v1>

**原始摘要**

LLM routing aims to choose, for each prompt, which model in a portfolio is most likely to answer correctly. This only makes sense when the benchmark contains enough prompt-specific variation for different models to be best on different inputs. On coarse-scored LLM benchmarks, however, many models often tie for the best score, and the single best model may already leave little room for improvement. We therefore propose a routeability-first audit, instantiated as RAP and RouteabilityBench, a runnable claim-verification package. The audit is retrospective: it runs once the benchmark-portfolio score matrix exists and before router comparisons are interpreted as general evidence, rather than certifying a portfolio that has not yet been evaluated. Across four benchmarks and 66 LLMs, generic routing enhancements such as larger embedding sets, dimensionality reduction, selective abstention, clustering, and stronger regressors do not consistently outperform the single best model. The clearest positive evidence is concentrated in portfolio-dependent Omni-MATH settings, while GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and IFEval show different ways in which routing can remain hard or predictor-sensitive. Our practical recommendation is that routing gains should be reported together with routeability diagnostics, tied-best analysis, portfolio construction details, and family-corrected statistical tests.

### 44. [The Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark: Do Language Models Know the Foundations of Early-Grade Literacy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34823v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：linguistic knowledge, early-grade literacy, knowledge benchmark, TWO BEST models under, kiswahili and the MEAN, KNOW whether THEY HOLD
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34823v1>

**原始摘要**

Many languages of instruction in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) are poorly represented in the data used to train language models. Before these models can support early-grade literacy in such languages, we need to know whether they hold the linguistic knowledge of each language that literacy teaching relies on. To test this, we present the Linguistic Knowledge Benchmark and the reproducible, largely automated pipeline that turns curriculum documents, teacher guides and pupil books into a vetted question set organised on a fixed grid of nine linguistic domains and seven grade levels. The pipeline combines LLM-driven source survey, feature consolidation, item generation and quality control with an item-selection step inspired by item response theory and a final expert review. We instantiate it for Kiswahili (Kenya; 135 items) and English (United Kingdom; 137 items) and evaluate 121 and 119 models respectively. Frontier models exceed 90% accuracy in both languages, but accuracy falls steeply for smaller and cheaper models, and more so in Kiswahili. The two best models under 10 billion parameters reach 91.7% and 73.0% in English but only 51.9% and 40.7% in Kiswahili, and the mean accuracy of models deployable on mobile and tablet hardware is 47% in English against 24% in Kiswahili. The models most practical for low-resource classrooms are therefore the least capable in the languages they would serve. We release a 20-item sample per language, all pipeline prompts, open-source code, and a public leaderboard. This is the first rung of a three-level evaluation ladder for measuring and generating decodable text to support foundational literacy.

### 45. [LoopTitan: What Does Parameter Revisit Buy a Mixture-of-Experts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34821v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：parameter revisit, MoE, WHAT DOES parameter, revisit itself, without revisit, BUY using matched non-revisit
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34821v1>

**原始摘要**

Looped Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures revisit layer parameters across depths. This design increases effective depth without adding parameters. Recent looped MoE studies report substantial gains over the standard MoE at matched budgets (parameters, FLOPs and KV cache). However, the reported gains still confound parameter revisit with other design choices, e.g., network shape and residual scale. We ask, what does parameter revisit itself buy? Using matched non-revisit controls, we find that parameter revisit itself buys almost nothing. We argue that revisit only offers a freedom an MoE already has, i.e., trading experts for depth (more layers, fewer experts each) at fixed parameters. First, at matched budgets, we decompose the state-of-the-art looped recipe, with revisit last. A non-revisit control retains all the gain. Most comes from network shape, and the rest from residual scale. We prove this scale acts as a lower learning rate on revisited layers. This helps under AdamW's uneven layer step sizes, but disappears under Muon, which keeps them nearly equal. Second, beyond matched budgets, we evaluate revisit by adding compute at fixed parameters. One extra visit gains more than doubling the experts. Yet without revisit, a deeper standard MoE at equal parameters and compute obtains 90.2% of that gain under Muon. Overall, gains come from network shape, the optimizer and added compute, which a standard MoE can adopt. Accordingly, we propose LoopTitan-Base as a stronger MoE baseline. Under Muon, it matches the strongest looped recipe without revisit or residual scale. We open-source LoopTitan and release 17 pretrained models (including LoopTitan-Base and the control). Our experiment record is public as GitHub issues, with predictions pre-registered and the full exploration history kept. This is our Phase 1 technical report, laying the foundation for follow-up studies.

### 46. [VulnSafe: Measuring Vulnerability-Aware Relational Safety in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34819v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：relational safety, large language models, context-appropriate support. existing safety, vulnerability-aware response competence VARC, vulnsafe measuring vulnerability-aware relational, language models NOT ONLY
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34819v1>

**原始摘要**

Emotionally supportive interactions require large language models not only to avoid overtly harmful content, but also to recognize vulnerability-related risk, regulate their relational stance, preserve user agency, and provide context-appropriate support. Existing safety benchmarks often measure isolated violations, leaving unclear what underlying response competence their scores represent. We introduce VulnSafe, a Chinese capability assessment centered on Vulnerability-Aware Response Competence (VARC). VARC organizes relational safety into five complementary constructs: Risk Perception, Safety Preservation, Autonomy Protection, Supportive Response, and Boundary Management. Following an evidence-centered design, 41 scenarios elicit construct-relevant behavior through 2,050 balanced prompts and scenario-specific scoring rubrics. The assessment further separates proactive should-do behavior from prohibitive should-not behavior. Across eight representative LLMs, results show distinct competence profiles and larger disparities than prohibitive requirements. VulnSafe therefore makes relational safety interpretable as a multidimensional model capability rather than as refusal compliance alone.

### 47. [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi-UAV Data Harvesting Under Variable Team Sizes and Agent Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, agent, multi-uav data harvesting under, team-size sampling and exposure, costly and fixed-population policies, learning for multi-uav
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818v1>

**原始摘要**

Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

### 48. [HOG-WAM: Improving Visual Robustness through Future Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：attention, diffusion, transformer, hog-wam which ADDS future, hog-wam achieves the highest, introduce hog-wam which ADDS
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811v1>

**原始摘要**

World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM's RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/HOG-WAM.

### 49. [WorldExpand: Building Coherent 3D Worlds via Sparse Joint Panoramic Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：joint panoramic, attention, diffusion, in-the-wild scenes. worldexpand RUNS, diverse in-the-wild scenes. worldexpand, sparse complementary and high-resolution
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810v1>

**原始摘要**

Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. This representation concentrates the generation budget on informative scene regions and preserves the fine-grained details required for 3D construction. WorldExpand addresses two key challenges: where to observe and how to synthesize. Our Coverage-Guided View Selection identifies informative target locations using scene-adaptive quadrants and warp-based coverage scores, while our Joint Panoramic Diffusion uses Gaussian-splatting-based geometric warps to jointly synthesize four pose-grounded and cross-view-consistent panoramas. A diagnostic-guided global--local attention schedule retains global attention only in sensitive layers, reducing the training and inference costs of high-resolution joint generation. The resulting posed panoramas can be directly fed into an existing reconstruction pipeline to build an explicit 3D representation. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

### 50. [Guidance as Variance Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：diffusion, introduce variance-matched guidance, picks the guidance scale, guidance DOES NOT improve, guidance each model makes, support guidance can WORK
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801v1>

**原始摘要**

Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model's training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior's covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

### 51. [Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, WHEN the split-rl score, reach the leaves. split-rl, split-rl leverages this BIAS, characterize WHEN the split-rl, split-rl localizes the tradeoff
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

### 52. [CIRKDV2: Cross-Image Relational Knowledge Distillation with Contextual Modeling for Efficient Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34795v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：cirkdv2, semantic segmentation, knowledge distillation, cross-image relational, semantic relations, feature distillation
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34795v1>

**原始摘要**

Existing Knowledge Distillation (KD) methods for semantic segmentation often guide the student to mimic the teacher's structured information generated from an individual image. However, they ignore the global semantic relations across various images that are valuable for KD. This paper proposes Cross-Image Relational KD V2 (CIRKDV2), which focuses on transferring structured pixel-to-pixel, pixel-to-region, and channel-to-channel relations modeled from global images in the dataset. A large teacher network often constructs a well-structured feature space in terms of global contextual dependencies. CIRKDV2 makes the student mimic the better structured semantic relations of the teacher, thus improving the feature representational capability. Beyond relational distillation, we further propose contextual feature distillation by using context aggregation to augment student pixel embeddings to match the semantic level with the teacher. This avoids the semantic gap problem between the teacher and student occurred in traditional feature distillation studies. Experimental results on Cityscapes, ADE20K, Pascal VOC, and COCO-Stuff-164K demonstrate that CIRKDV2 outperforms the state-of-the-art semantic segmentation distillation methods by large margins across various network architectures. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/winycg/CIRKD.

### 53. [PersonaFactory: Stylistic User Prompt Augmentation with Synthetic Persona Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34785v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：USER prompts, alignment, large language models, fidelity THAN free-text persona, THAN free-text persona descriptions, roughly million persona-styled synthetic
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34785v1>

**原始摘要**

Large language models increasingly replace real people in the AI development pipeline, as they are used to generate training dialogues at scale and to drive alignment and evaluation. However, out-of-the-box models produce monolingual and stylistically flat texts as synthetic user prompts, failing to replicate the real user-AI communication mechanics. We present PersonaFactory, an end-to-end pipeline that generates detailed user personas and uses them to augment existing user prompts from their point of view, producing a bank of over 740000 structured synthetic personas detailing their background and communication behaviors, and a corpus of roughly 1.24 million persona-styled synthetic conversations spanning 85 languages. We evaluate the effect of personal features on style adoption and intent preservation, using an LLM-judge and heuristic metrics to assess augmentation fidelity together with the downstream impact on assistant persona control through training data. Results show that our structured, attribute-based style representation enables per-axis control and better intent preservation at comparable style fidelity than free-text persona descriptions used in existing methods. A human expert evaluation found the augmented conversations plausible, with favorable ratings for intent preservation, style naturalness, and multilingual authenticity. Finally, PersonaFactory conversations achieve comparable performance to original training data on capability benchmarks, are preferred on real user prompts at equal reply length, and adapt their reply style more closely to users without explicit steering.

### 54. [Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Brain Tumour Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34781v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：DEEP learning, learning architectures, attention, efficientnet-b0, architectures for four-class classification, four-class classification healthy meningioma
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34781v1>

**原始摘要**

Accurate and early classification of brain tumours from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for treatment planning, yet many published deep learning studies are trained and tested on small, single-source datasets, reporting accuracies that may not hold outside these datasets. This study compares six deep learning architectures for four-class classification (healthy, meningioma, glioma and pituitary tumour): a baseline convolutional neural network (CNN), an attention-based CNN, ShuffleNet, and EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50 and Xception augmented with the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM). The models were trained on a corpus assembled from four publicly available MRI datasets. Because these datasets carry no patient identifiers, a leakage-aware pipeline was developed that removes exact duplicates using MD5 hashing (550 images) and near-duplicates using perceptual hashing (8,024 images), and assigns visually similar images to the same fold during 5-fold group cross-validation. A leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) test on a fully held-out dataset was used to measure cross-dataset generalisation. Under a conventional random split, ShuffleNet achieved 97.1% accuracy with 1.2M parameters. Under leakage-aware group K-fold cross-validation, ShuffleNetV2 (98.41%) and EfficientNet-B0 (98.23%) were statistically indistinguishable (paired t-test, p = 0.19), and both significantly outperformed the custom CNNs. On the held-out dataset, accuracy dropped for every model: EfficientNet-B0 generalised best (78.4%), while ShuffleNetV2 fell to 66.2%. Glioma-meningioma confusion was the dominant error mode, and Grad-CAM maps showed that predictions were driven by tumour regions. These results indicate that in-distribution accuracy alone overstates real-world performance and that leakage-aware and cross-dataset evaluation should be reported alongside it.

### 55. [Adaptive Reliability Reserves for Residential Battery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34779v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：residential battery, benchmark, ALSO needed for household, warning for every battery, battery programs that assess, continuous warning for every
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34779v1>

**原始摘要**

In a Houston 2024 replay, replacing a fixed 20 percent battery reserve with a county-risk threshold raises observed five-hour backup coverage from 75.0 percent to 86.5 percent and adds USD 10.2 in modeled annualized margin per home (paired 95 percent interval: USD +1.7 to USD +21.7). The paired coverage-gain interval includes zero. This is a conditional simulation result, not measured company reliability. Residential batteries face this tradeoff because market discharge consumes energy also needed for household outages. We release HomeFleet-ERCOT, combining observed prices and county outages with identified simulated homes and causal controller inputs. We compare independent-home optimization with 21 proximal policy optimization (PPO) training runs; ten promise-cost candidates span 31.1 percent-96.3 percent backup coverage. Of these, 10 have lower modeled margin than fixed 20 percent, and 3 also have lower coverage. Chronological, geographic, fleet, and storm tests examine transfer. An alert charging override has a conditional energy-and-power sufficiency guarantee, but only 19.5 percent of 41 county mass-outage episodes provide enough continuous warning for every battery preset. Synthetic tests include short, missed, and false warnings, load uplift, capacity loss, and dropout. A static AC audit retains existing feeder violations; aging comparisons expose model sensitivity. The released benchmark, controllers, and checkpoints provide a reproducible basis for comparing these tradeoffs. This work supports the design of residential battery programs that assess market value and household backup service together.

### 56. [Toward Agentic Robotics: A Large-Scale Study of Harness Design for Robot-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34778v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：harness design, reasoning, vision-language, design for robot-use agents., robot-use agents that understand, VLA WAM
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34778v1>

**原始摘要**

Recent vision-language models (VLMs) can act as robot-use agents that understand diverse scenes and control robots through policy code and tools. However, they predict 3D gripper poses poorly even with extended reasoning, struggling with precise manipulation. Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models are the opposite: they execute fast, smooth control on trained tasks, yet fail under domain shifts or when tasks require long-horizon memory and reasoning. Agentic robots therefore combine VLM reasoning and perception with precise VLA/WAM control through a policy harness. Yet harness design spans thousands of configurations, shifting success by tens of points with the same models. Moreover, testing even one design takes thousands of rollouts at high API and GPU cost, so prior works often commit to a single design chosen from few alternatives. To guide harness design, we evaluate over 470,000 rollouts across eight simulation benchmarks, six VLMs, and 15 VLA/WAM checkpoints. Seven controlled studies show effective harnesses must adapt to both VLM and VLA/WAM capabilities. Our findings specify what structure the VLM's policy code needs, where VLA manipulation needs help from tools and policy code, and when to involve the VLM at test time. The reference recipe X-Harness shows that these findings compose without weight updates, lifting base policies up to $+42.8$ points across memory, reasoning, and domain shifts.

### 57. [DFly2: Turbocharging Long-Context Speculative Decoding by Sharing the Target's Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34776v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：dfly2, long-context and agentic, speculative decoding, attention, recipe OVER general long-context, turbocharging long-context speculative
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34776v1>

**原始摘要**

To avoid the quadratic cost of dense attention over long contexts, models such as DeepSeek-V4 and Hy4 have turned natively sparse, and speculative decoding, which matters most where decoding is slowest, increasingly runs on them. Their drafters, however, still keep a context cache of their own, over the whole prompt or a sliding window. Beyond memory, such a cache costs prefill time, prefix-cache hits and decode concurrency. Drafters that read the target's cache avoid one of their own, but settle only which cache, not which positions, and still read everything or a recent window. Neither rule is made for the query at hand, and computing a selection per query in the drafter would cost more as the context grows. Yet a natively sparse target already picks positions for each query, over a cache it keeps anyway. Both of the drafter's choices are therefore already made, and paid for, by the target: its cache and its selection. We measure how far one selection carries: made a step early, it still covers close to nine tenths of what each later token selects on DS-V4.1-Flash. We present DFly2, a block-parallel drafter that shares the target's sparsity. Each draft layer reads the KV its paired target layer cached (KV sharing), at the positions that layer's indexer selected, a fixed number anywhere in the context (Index sharing). DFly2 thus runs no scoring pass and keeps no cache of its own. On DS-V4.1-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash and Hy4~flash, whose sparse attention and architecture differ, it accepts more tokens per step than DFlash and DSpark trained on the same recipe, over general, long-context and agentic benchmarks. Its margin over the stronger baseline widens from 3.9%-10.5% on general workloads to 10.0%-31.5% on long-context and agentic ones. At high concurrency on DS-V4.1-Flash, at matched per-user decode speed, its throughput is 62% and 81% higher on two 64K sets.

### 58. [Anytime-Valid Sequential Verification for Sensing-Efficient Robot Navigation under Perception Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34764v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：sequential GATE, sensing-efficient robot navigation under, verification for sensing-efficient robot, sequential verification for sensing-efficient, anytime-valid sequential verification, versus for the original
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34764v1>

**原始摘要**

When a robot navigates an unfamiliar grid, noisy sensor readings can make it hard to tell whether the cells along its next move are free. Additional scans can reduce that uncertainty, but a fixed scan count spends the same sensing effort at every position.
