In a static-grid simulation, a sequential rule uses a Gaussian likelihood ratio to determine when the cells newly covered by the robot's footprint have enough evidence of free space. On 324 held-out scenarios from 18 public maps, the sequential gate and a four-scan reference chosen on validation data both completed every mission without an observed collision. With their respective validation-selected routing weights, the gate averaged 123.15 scans per episode versus 224.13 for the reference, a reduction of 45.05% (95% map-stratified bootstrap interval: 42.21--48.95%). In a retrospective check on the same scenarios with routing weights held equal, the reductions were 43.91% and 43.37% at the two tested weights, with all missions completed. On 216 scenarios from 12 previously unused maps, allocating more of the same certification error budget near the initial start--goal rectangle cut the sequential gate's scan count by a further 11.58%. The spatial variant completed 206 missions versus 208 for the original gate and increased computation time. The collision bound depends on the assumed observation model and footprint geometry. In a stress test with persistent per-cell bias, the original gate completed 0 of 54 missions.

### 59. [TASK Memory: Beyond Text-Only Memory with Activation Steering and Knowledge Editing against Memory Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：beyond text-only memory, steering and knowledge editing, activation steering and knowledge, LLM agents, external memory, memory library OVER bm25
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762v1>

**原始摘要**

LLM agents increasingly rely on external memory, yet they still hallucinate about what they remember. Often the correct memory is retrieved and placed in the prompt, but the answer contradicts it or the memory is applied to the wrong question. We call these use-time memory hallucinations. Across five open models, we find that they arise from the conflict between parametric knowledge and external memory and take three forms: memory-ignoring, where the model returns to a stale fact in its weights (up to 46% of queries); memory-misapplying, where it attributes the memory to unrelated entities (up to 24%, and 49% when retrieval is combined with weight editing); and version confusion, where 53% of answers about revised facts assert the superseded value when both versions remain stored. We propose TASK memory, which goes beyond text-only memory by combining a Text note with Activation Steering and Knowledge editing that share one in-model address. The text holds the content. A removable knowledge edit supplies the address through its subject key and supports multi-hop composition. A pair of steering vectors decides, query by query, whether the model adopts the memory or keeps its own answer; the vectors store no content and control how the text memory is used. On frozen models under a pre-registered, test-once protocol, TASK reduces memory-ignoring hallucinations to at most 1.5% in all ten model-dataset cells and cuts neighbour misuse from 39.2% to 7.5% (+10.8 pp composite on CounterFact). It improves a 300-memory library over BM25 RAG by 16.4 pp, an advantage that holds at 3,000 memories, resolves fact revisions without fact IDs, and adds 7.5 pp of multi-hop accuracy on MQuAKE. 21 of 23 cross-model comparisons (8B-27B, four families) are significant, and TASK beats three recent context-steering methods in 9 of 10 model-dataset cells.

### 60. [One Model, Many Modes: Evolving System Prompt Ensembles for Creative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34760v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：system prompt, creative generation, prompt ensemble, attention, training-free framework for system, ONE model many modes
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34760v1>

**原始摘要**

Preference-aligned LLMs can exhibit mode collapse, concentrating their output distributions around a narrow set of high-reward responses. This limits creative generation, which requires quality, novelty, and diversity. Existing test-time methods often emphasize diversity, giving less attention to whether individual responses are both high-quality and novel. This distinction matters in practical use, where users may seek a single compelling, original response. We formalize creative generation as a multi-objective distributional optimization problem and introduce SEPO, a training-free framework for System Prompt Ensemble Optimization. The framework combines prompt evolution with greedy max-min selection to identify behaviorally distinct system prompts under a quality constraint, then uniformly samples one prompt per response at inference time. Across four creative generation tasks with increasing quality requirements and three open-weight models, SEPO ranks first in 8 of 12 settings on the harmonic mean of quality, diversity, and novelty, while no baseline leads in more than two. On average, it raises LLM-judged diversity from 1.92 to 3.43 and novelty from 1.00 to 3.34 over base sampling (1-5 scale), with quality decreasing from 3.72 to 3.30. We further show that expanding the optimized ensemble increases separation among prompt-conditioned response distributions. These results support system prompt ensemble optimization as a promising approach to improving LLM creativity at inference time.

### 61. [Reading Fine-Tuning Diffs Through Lenses: Model Diffing for Backdoor Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34758v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-tuning, backdoor from the weight-based, output-based model diffing methods, weight-based methods, BASE model which ranks, naming the backdoor among
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34758v1>

**原始摘要**

We introduce a common scoring system based on token readouts to evaluate weight, activation and output-based model diffing methods for backdoor detection in fine-tuning processes. We also improve existing weight and activation based methods by incorporating the $J$-lens and its refinement, the $J{+}{+}$-lens. We test our setup in experiments where we compare a backdoored model with the base model from which it was fine-tuned. Our results show that incorporating the $J{+}{+}$-lens improves the weight-based method in all our settings, while the $J$-lens improves its ranks within single layers. The strongest single method is $ΔW J{+}{+}$, the weight difference decoded through the $J{+}{+}$-lens of the base model, which ranks a token naming the backdoor among its first ten tokens in 11 of our 12 settings. Moreover, we show that output and activation based methods such as ADL and Diff Mining can easily break if the backdoored model is penalised during fine-tuning for giving the backdoor's words probability on clean inputs, whereas the weight-based methods are not affected. Conversely, permuting the hidden units or training only the layers that read the residual stream hides the backdoor from the weight-based methods but not from ADL and Diff Mining. No single method resists both attacks.

### 62. [Collect for Coverage, Replay with Evidence: Adaptive and Efficient On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34756v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：prefix replay, collect for coverage replay, coverage replay with evidence, on-policy distillation, history coverage, IDLE TIME for long-tail
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34756v1>

**原始摘要**

Fully online on-policy distillation (OPD) is costly for long-horizon agents because each update depends on fresh environment rollouts. Prefix replay offers an efficient alternative by sampling current-student responses from stored histories, reducing idle time for long-tail rollouts and allowing each collected trajectory to support multiple updates. However, our analysis of the complete prefix replay pipeline identifies two limitations in long-horizon settings. In trajectory collection, teacher-only trajectories miss student-induced histories, while broader history coverage can reduce teacher reliability. In trajectory reuse, fixed position-based schedules cannot identify critical decisions that may occur at any trajectory depth. We introduce CARE-OPD (Collect for coverAge, Replay with Evidence) to address both limitations. Student-seeded teacher completions and response-level confidence gating expand history coverage while filtering unreliable updates. Hindsight replay allocation then uses downstream execution evidence to select consequential turns. Across long-horizon tasks Software Engineering and Deep Research, CARE-OPD outperforms online OPD and prior prefix replay baselines. On SWE-bench Verified, it raises task success to 59.5% while reducing end-to-end training time by 45% and environment actions by 44% relative to asynchronous online OPD.

### 63. [SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34750v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：LLM agents, versatile and efficient, sparse ordered, multimodal, DOF end-effector keyposes PER, step-by-step control and TOOL
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34750v1>

**原始摘要**

LLM agents have made remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet they suffer from expensive inference, dense step-by-step control, and tool bottlenecks. We introduce SOLVE, a training-free, versatile and efficient agent policy: a multimodal LLM agent writes a sparse, ordered sequence of 6-DoF end-effector keyposes per call, observes the result, and writes the next, while a motion planner interpolates between them. The agent decides the number and spacing of keyposes for both fast long-range motion and fine-grained manipulation. This granularity matches the sparsity of teleoperated manipulation: most demonstrations in DROID can be compressed into a few keyposes instead of dense actions. SOLVE grounds keyposes with sparse depth queries answered by noisy sources, such as multi-view triangulation, stereo, or monocular depth, and copes with that noise itself. It also improves from execution feedback, reflecting on its own rollouts and distilling a few rules per task. SOLVE reaches state-of-the-art performance of 74.5% on RoboDojo and 84.5% on RoboLab, with only $1.7 per episode on average, 12x less than previous dense-control agent policies. On a real robot, the same agent achieves an 88% success rate (75 of 85 episodes) over 16 challenging zero-shot tasks. Our project page is at https://serenos.github.io/solve-website.

### 64. [BRIAR: Martingale Belief Smoothing on Bifurcation-Guided Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34746v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reasoning, reinforcement learning, belief smoothing, rauch--tung--striebel RTS TREE belief, bifurcation-guided rollout and inference, introduce briar bifurcation-guided rollout
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34746v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has rapidly advanced long-chain reasoning, yet standard policy optimizers distribute rollout compute and gradient credit blindly across a trajectory. On hard problems, an early misstep derails the remaining trace, whereas on solvable problems, the policy keeps generating redundant verification long after the answer is already determined. We trace both pathologies to a single structural fact: under an on-policy verifier, the true prefix pass probability $V^π(u)$ along a reasoning trace forms a $[0,1]$-bounded Doob martingale whose learnable credit variance is strictly conserved and bounded above by $V^π(u)(1-V^π(u))$, vanishing at both the derailed ($V^π\to 0$) and absorbed ($V^π\to 1$) boundaries. We introduce BRIAR (Bifurcation-guided Rollout and Inference on Adaptive Reasoning trees), a tree-structured RLVR framework built directly around this martingale geometry. First, BRIAR expands discourse-aligned reasoning trees along an upper-confidence outcome-bifurcation frontier, steering rollouts away from absorbed rumination and toward unresolved decision points. Second, it models latent node values with boundary-scaled heteroscedastic edge variances and computes exact global posteriors in linear $\mathcal{O}(|V|)$ time via two-pass Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) tree belief smoothing, eliminating $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ matrix inversions while gating out spurious boundary noise. Third, BRIAR detects minimal absorbed frontier nodes and applies zero-sum log-length centered suffix advantages strictly post-absorption, leaving pre-absorption derivations provably invariant.

### 65. [How Well Do Vision-Language Scene Graphs Serve as Memory for Embodied Question Answering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34745v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：vision-language, real-video TEST graph memory, needs memory BUT answer, TEST graph memory lowers, memory BUT answer accuracy, errors and question priors.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34745v1>

**原始摘要**

Embodied QA needs memory, but answer accuracy mixes retained evidence, reading errors and question priors. For a simulated multi-room scene-graph pipeline we define answerability: the fraction of questions a fixed rule-based reader answers correctly from the graph alone. The baseline recovers 24.1% of visible object types and its room labels flicker. Four targeted repairs double recall, raise answerability from 27.2% to 37.4%, and improve retrained QA from 71.4% to 75.9%, yet better object F1 can lower answerability. A question-type prior beats this reader on every predicted graph. In one real-video test, graph memory lowers accuracy. These exploratory findings favour scoring retained evidence alongside accuracy.

### 66. [ELMO: Emergent Representations In Imitation Learning Via Dynamic Hierarchical Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34741v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, emergent latent-action model ELMO, introduce the emergent latent-action, representations that ground task-level, representations are context-agnostic motion, action chunks into latent
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34741v1>

**原始摘要**

Robot trajectories naturally decompose into variable-length phases with distinct semantic intents, such as reaching, grasping, and lifting. However, training policies to minimize MSE treats all actions as equal, obscuring their semantic structure. While latent action models attempt to create a structured prediction target by compressing action chunks into latent representations, existing approaches rely on two-stage training and context-blind decoders. Consequently, their representations are context-agnostic motion codes (e.g., "move down") which fail to capture behavioural intent (e.g., "pick up the block"). To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce the Emergent Latent-action MOdel (ELMO), an end-to-end framework that encodes robot trajectories as grounded semantic codes -- representations that ground task-level behavioural intent in the current environment. ELMO jointly trains a VLA planner and an observation-conditioned decoder over dynamically chunked trajectories, learning a semantically grounded action space without separate pretraining. Across standard benchmarks, ELMO outperforms comparable prior methods by 9.6 percentage points on LIBERO-Plus and by 0.75 in average task length on CALVIN. Furthermore, on Blocks -- our shortcut-free simulation and real-world generalization benchmark -- ELMO reaches a 38.6% success rate while all baselines remain below 5%, demonstrating its ability to capture the semantic structure of demonstrations. Website: https://uoft-isl.github.io/elmo.github.io/

### 67. [Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：navigation world, world models, ANY action-conditioned world, NWM and FIND three, WAS the model given, prepared the SAME WAY
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739v1>

**原始摘要**

World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

### 68. [From NP-Hardness to Exact Tractability Through Sample Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34734v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：exact optimization, observations can MAKE exact, exact tractability through sample, through sample abundance. additional, tractability through sample abundance., sample abundance. additional observations
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34734v1>

**原始摘要**

Additional observations can make exact optimization tractable without changing the objective or feasible set. We establish this phenomenon for noisy parity fitting, a worst-case NP-hard problem, with uniformly sampled rank-one sign tensor entries, independent fair factor signs and independent errors. For fixed interaction order $k\ge3$, $n$ signs per block, logarithmic mean degree and known channel biases, $O(n/(\log n)^{1/(k-2)})$ further readings of the same source suffice with high probability for exact optimization in $O(n\log^2 n)$ worst-case work. The leading coefficient is sharp for weak recovery by depth-$O(\log\log n)$ local estimators with independent reading marks. A common signed-field law determines the gain over the generating source, the number of tied optima and the dimension needed for later reweightings. The geometry of the remaining coordinates determines the sharp radius of local verification for high-scoring assignments with high probability.

### 69. [WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：video generation, streaming video, world models, multi-material interactions. video world, generation for multi-material interactions., worldinteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730v1>

**原始摘要**

Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

### 70. [Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34726v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：graph metanetworks, hierarchical graph, metanetworks h-gmns which leverage, including message-passing based graph, graph using permutation-symmetry-preserving pooling, graph construction that preserves
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34726v1>

**原始摘要**

The emerging field of weight space learning has demonstrated the potential to enable metanetworks, i.e. models that process neural network (NN) data, to support several steps of the machine learning lifecycle, from model optimisation to evaluation and adaptation. However, this potential has yet to materialise beyond small-scale MLPs or CNNs: most current models, including message-passing based graph metanetworks (GMNs), operate exclusively at the finest level of granularity, processing every neuron and weight of the input model. Their cost therefore scales with the number of parameters. Yet NNs are inherently organised hierarchically, with neurons forming layers and layers forming blocks and modules. Additionally, several of their properties, such as Lipschitz upper bounds and generalisation measures, are compositions of layer- and block-level quantities. To incorporate this inductive bias, we introduce Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks (H-GMNs), which leverage the input architecture's hierarchy to coarsen and refine the graph using permutation-symmetry-preserving pooling and un-pooling layers. Further, we extend H-GMNs to Transformers by introducing a graph construction that preserves permutation symmetries and enables multi-level pooling. Our evaluation includes new ResNet-18, ViT and varying ViT model zoos of up to 86M parameters, on tasks spanning model evaluation (an analytic Lipschitz bound proxy, generalisation and robustness prediction), testing (backdoor detection) and editing (pruning). Experimental results show that, compared to non-equivariant alternatives and flat GMNs, H-GMNs are markedly more sample-efficient, and their cost remains nearly constant as metanetwork depth grows, allowing deeper and more expressive processing where flat GMNs run out of memory, without compromising accuracy, which in certain cases considerably improves.

### 71. [LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction with Limited Real Data: An Architecture Benchmark under Fixed-Epoch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：lidar intensity, intensity reconstruction, benchmark, diffusion, unet-v2 leads with REAL, REAL frames. with real-only
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724v1>

**原始摘要**

Physics-derived simulation can supplement scarce real-world LiDAR intensity measurements, but its effectiveness depends on the learning architecture and the amount of real training data available. We benchmark nine neural architectures for LiDAR intensity reconstruction from depth, semantic labels, incidence angle, and material reflectance, using real SemanticKITTI and synthetic VoxelScape range-image data. All models follow a fixed 20-epoch protocol; this controls the number of training passes, not the computational cost across architectures. We report mean absolute error (MAE), mean squared error (MSE), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity (SSIM), inference timing, and spectral-energy agreement. We also evaluate four selected architectures with fixed-size training mixtures containing 0\%, 1\%, 5\%, 10\%, 20\%, 50\%, or 100\% real frames. With real-only training, UNet-V2 achieves the best reported reconstruction metrics (MAE 0.234, MSE 0.140, PSNR 8.64 dB, and SSIM 0.647), followed by a denoising diffusion model (DDPM). In several mixtures containing 1--10\% real data, DDPM achieves lower error and higher image-quality metrics than the evaluated deterministic alternatives, whereas UNet-V2 leads with 100\% real data. Spectral analysis additionally reveals architecture-dependent discrepancies across frequency bands. These findings characterize reconstruction trade-offs under the stated data mixtures and fixed-epoch protocol, without establishing compute-matched superiority or isolating the diffusion objective from backbone differences.

### 72. [Robust Recourse under Causal Graph Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34723v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：causal graph, benchmark, trade-off. accounting for graph, ambiguity can HELP decision-makers, recourse and graph-weighted recourse, worst-case recourse and graph-weighted
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34723v1>

**原始摘要**

Causal algorithmic recourse recommends actions to reverse unfavorable decisions by modeling how interventions change an individual's features. Existing methods typically assume one causal graph that describes the system and account for uncertainty in the causal mechanisms. However, there usually exist several graphs that fit the same data but can predict different effects for the same action. Certain actions may achieve recourse across multiple graphs, but as the set of potential graphs grows, a single best action may become infeasible. We propose Worst-Case Recourse and Graph-Weighted Recourse to find a low-cost action that succeeds across all candidate graphs or a specified fraction of graph weight, respectively. Assuming a set of plausible graphs, for each graph we fit Gaussian process structural equations and assess success using the posterior mean--standard-deviation criterion. We use gradient-based search to determine minimal cost actions and check the validity of the actions on fresh posterior samples. In synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks, relaxing robustness, the requirement that an action succeed under every candidate graph, can increase the number of valid recommendations for recourse and lower costs of actions for both methods. In the 20-node GP benchmark, relaxation increases the number of factual instances offered recourse (coverage) from 341 to 436 out of 1,000, while the number of true successes, actions that flip the decision under the generating model, falls from 251 to 184, illustrating the coverage--validity trade-off. Accounting for graph ambiguity can help decision-makers recommend actions that remain effective even when the causal mechanisms of the system are not fully known.

### 73. [Privileged On-Policy Distillation for Calibrated Alzheimer's Diagnosis With Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34721v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：on-policy distillation, BOTH GAPS with on-policy, well-calibrated model for whatever, level with the lowest, WHAT the missing tests, missing tests leave OPEN
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34721v1>

**原始摘要**

Alzheimer's disease is diagnosed from clinical, biospecimen and imaging evidence, but few patients undergo every test. Because these modalities are complementary, a missing one has many plausible values. Most existing methods leave it out or train on one estimate, so their classifier never sees its other values, and the estimate is never checked against the diagnosis. We address both gaps with on-policy distillation: the model fills in the missing modalities itself, and teachers with information available only in training grade these fills. Our method, Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers, draws several fills, one modality at a time. An imputation teacher that knows the diagnosis grades each step, bringing the outcome into the imputation as statistical practice recommends. A diagnosis teacher that sees all real modalities supplies a soft target. Trained on fills that vary, the model learns a confidence that reflects what the missing tests leave open, and it diagnoses from a single rollout. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On two external cohorts, it remains competitive in discrimination and, even after every method is recalibrated, its calibration error is level with the lowest on both. Added to three of these baselines, the teachers improve each of them. Our method offers a single, well-calibrated model for whatever combination of tests a patient has received.

### 74. [Reading, Not Adapting: Unlocking Frozen Visual Knowledge for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34719v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：frozen visual, transformer, attention, benchmark, foundation models, knowledge for ai-generated image
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34719v1>

**原始摘要**

Generalization to unseen image generators has driven efforts to adapt pretrained visual representations for detection. Yet recent findings show that vision foundation models already encode transferable detection knowledge, while further adaptation can impair generalization. This motivates a shift from backbone adaptation to knowledge readout. Conventional linear probing offers a direct way to read this knowledge but is limited to final-layer RGB features. Multi-cue detectors broaden the evidence base, but their designs introduce other tradeoffs: adaptation may compromise pretrained transferability, heterogeneous encoders complicate feature integration, and late fusion precludes intermediate cross-view interaction. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers-inspired framework that reads complementary knowledge from spatially aligned RGB and wavelet views within a fully frozen visual foundation. ReMoT employs staged joint attention to enable controlled local and global cross-view interaction, while learnable fusion queries progressively read complementary evidence across Transformer depth without writeback. ReMoT achieves 91.2% average accuracy across eight benchmark groups with 3,074 trainable parameters, attaining the highest overall average among the evaluated methods. These results support structured knowledge readout as a promising alternative to backbone adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/yunncheng/ReMoT.

### 75. [From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：emerging MRR, shared patterns, emerging entities, temporal knowledge, individual evidence, reasoning
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708v1>

**原始摘要**

Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

### 76. [Maco-RL: Scaling Agent Capabilities with Mixed-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34703v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, mixed-task reinforcement, agent capabilities, distillation, FIVE benchmarks show maco-rl, benchmarks show maco-rl raises
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34703v1>

**原始摘要**

Mixed-task reinforcement learning (MixRL) directly trains a unified policy across domains to continually expand agent capabilities without training separate specialists or requiring an extra distillation stage. Despite its appeal, scaling MixRL poses significant challenges in achieving stable and efficient mixed-task learning, effectively allocating rollout compute, and supporting continual domain expansion. To address these challenges, we propose Maco-RL, an approach that effectively scales MixRL. Specifically, Maco-RL employs a quota-based scheduler for asynchronous rollouts across multiple task domains, thereby stabilizing training by balancing gradient contributions and mitigating off-policy effects. Along an orthogonal axis, Maco-RL further introduces Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS) to allocate compute among samples with varying levels of mastery, promoting sustained frontier exploration while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on five benchmarks show Maco-RL raises Qwen3.5-27B's overall score from 43.6 to 54.2 and then to 60.1 with continual expansion, reaching the frontier held by Qwen3.8-27B, which is further pushed outward by +5.7. Maco-RL also outperforms single-task RL with less compute for policy updates. These results support MixRL as a practical and compute-efficient post-training approach with substantial potential to strengthen general agent capabilities.

### 77. [Seeing the Right Context: Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：on-policy distillation, remote sensing, multimodal, large language models, right context grounded on-policy, outperforms the evaluated open-source
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700v1>

**原始摘要**

Remote sensing scene understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret queried targets in relation to nearby objects and surrounding structures, which serve as spatial references or define the scope of an answer. However, standard answers and bounding boxes often leave these contextual relationships implicit. Thus, we construct GeoContext-5K to make them explicit in 5,394 training pairs for visual question answering (VQA), with spatial cues, visual facts, and supporting regions. GeOPD uses these annotations for on-policy distillation, guiding a frozen teacher with region marks on the full image and a visual fact while preserving surrounding spatial references. It prioritizes teacher corrections that agree with the shift in the student's next token distribution when evidence is restored. A complementary objective weights the contrast between the student's scores on the two views by how much masking lowers the teacher's log probability for the same sampled token. Experiments on five remote sensing VQA benchmarks show that GeOPD outperforms the evaluated open-source MLLMs and matched post-training baselines, improving over the 4B and 9B base models by 5.81 and 2.97 percentage points on average. The dataset and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/YeeCharm/GeOPD.

### 78. [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：fine-grained speculative streaming, world models, roles for fine-grained commitment, fine-grained commitment and advance, complementary roles for fine-grained, long-horizon consistency. shownow turns
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693v1>

**原始摘要**

Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: https://github.com/showlab/ShowNow.

### 79. [PoVi: Agentic Poetry-to-Video Generation with Knowledge Grounding and Figurative Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34690v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：poetry-to-video generation, benchmark, multimodal, text-to-video, assets and shot-level visual, imagery and other non-literal
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34690v1>

**原始摘要**

Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. Poetic language is highly compressed, often depends on historical and cultural knowledge, and frequently conveys meaning through metaphor, imagery, and other non-literal expressions. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. PoVi provides a testbed for studying knowledge-grounded, long-horizon multimodal generation beyond direct text-to-video synthesis.

### 80. [Evacuation Traffic Prediction for Dynamic Transportation Networks over Multiple Hurricanes: A Generalized Graph Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34687v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：evacuation traffic, graph learning, HOUR prediction, multiple hurricanes., transportation networks, dynamic gcn-lstm model compared
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34687v1>

**原始摘要**

High traffic demand during hurricane evacuations causes severe congestion on major highways, leading to delays and safety risks for evacuees. Predicting evacuation traffic in real-time can significantly enhance evacuation management efforts. However, evacuation traffic is highly dynamic and uncertain due to evolving hurricane characteristics and diverse population response. Each hurricane has unique characteristics and evacuation contexts, resulting in different spatial-temporal traffic patterns. Moreover, the structure of detector-based transportation networks varies across multiple hurricanes. Hence, a model trained on the data from one hurricane may not generalize well for others. This study proposes a generalized dynamic graph learning framework using traffic detectors and evacuation data from 12 historical hurricanes. Leveraging the dynamic nature of transportation network, we develop a Graph Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (GCN-LSTM) model that predicts evacuation traffic up to 6 hours in advance. For an unseen hurricane (Milton, 2024), the model shows strong generalizability, achieving an RMSE of 280.6 and R2 of 0.96 for 1-hour prediction, and an RMSE of 459.8 and R2 of 0.89 for overall 6-hours prediction. We also introduce a modular training approach, where we evaluate performance improvements by sequentially incorporating data from multiple hurricanes. The proposed model outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models for overall 6-hour prediction horizons. Ablation experiments show an 8.4% improvement in RMSE for the proposed dynamic GCN-LSTM model compared to its static counterpart. Traffic management officials can use the developed model to understand future traffic conditions during hurricane evacuation in advance and take proactive measures to reduce delays during evacuation.

### 81. [Find the Clue, Not the Scene: Rethinking Reasoning for Entity Recognition in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34681v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：entity recognition, entity-recall CLUE, reinforcement learning, reasoning, vision-language, multimodal
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34681v1>

**原始摘要**

Answering a knowledge-based visual question requires first recognizing the entity in the image and then recalling knowledge about it. Thinking vision-language models (VLMs) reason for thousands of tokens before answering, yet it remains underexplored how well such thinking VLMs perform each of these two steps. We therefore analyze thinking VLMs on this task and make two key observations. (1) Recognition is the bottleneck, since 76.3% of errors across four models on ViQuAE stem from misrecognizing the entity, and 84.9% of these failures are resolved once the entity name is provided in the question. (2) Recognition is driven by entity-recall clues rather than by general descriptions of the image. An entity-recall clue is a distinctive visual detail that helps the model recall the entity, such as keys, a traditional attribute of Saint Peter. Based on these findings, we propose ClueRL, a reinforcement learning approach that extends Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an advantage localized to the entity-recall clue in the thinking trace. Rather than injecting new entity knowledge, ClueRL changes how VLMs think: it leads them to attend more closely to visual details and spot these clues, helping them use the knowledge they already have. Consequently, it outperforms both GRPO and a perception-reward baseline on three entity recognition benchmarks. Moreover, this clue-seeking behavior generalizes beyond entity recognition, the only task used for training. Since recognition is the bottleneck, the gains carry over to knowledge-based visual question answering. They even extend to general multimodal reasoning, raising Qwen3.5-4B by 4.4 and 4.0 points on the ScienceQA and MMMU subsets of MMStar, respectively.

### 82. [Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：generative video, neural rendering compiler, benchmark, text-to-video, video generation, video models are becoming
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. NeurCo improves requirement satisfaction from 46.8% to 52.3% on Wan and from 66.5% to 69.3% on Minimax H3, and achieves further gains when combined with prompt enhancers, including on Seedance 2.5. Our analysis reveals model-specific differences in control capabilities and trade-offs between controllability and photorealism, highlighting capability-guided compilation as a promising approach to programmable generative video rendering.

### 83. [Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, concave utility, linear function approximation., utility policy, utility reinforcement, log-linear policies with sufficiently
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678v1>

**原始摘要**

Concave utility reinforcement learning (CURL) unifies a diverse range of problems, including standard reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning, and maximum entropy exploration. Unlike standard RL, CURL generally requires estimating high-dimensional state-action occupancy measures. Consequently, existing methods for CURL either sidestep the exploration problem, are restricted to the tabular setting, or lack computationally efficient policy optimization. To address this, we study CURL in finite-horizon episodic bilinear MDPs, where the transition model admits a low-dimensional representation in known feature spaces. We use the bilinear structure to efficiently estimate occupancy measures via low-dimensional feature expectations, and propose a model-based optimistic concave utility policy optimization (OCUPO) framework. We first instantiate OCUPO in the on-policy setting, where each iteration uses independent trajectories to estimate the concave utility policy gradient, and prove an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity. To improve sample efficiency, we then instantiate OCUPO in the off-policy setting. We propose a novel backward feature regression subroutine that reuses trajectories collected by past policies to estimate the occupancy measure of the current policy. Under suitable technical assumptions on the policy class (satisfied, e.g., by log-linear policies with sufficiently expressive features), we prove that the resulting algorithm achieves an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity, up to a controllable projection error. We further extend our framework to linear MDPs and show that the resulting off-policy algorithm retains the same sample complexity. In these settings, the dependence on $ε$ matches that for standard RL with linear function approximation. Moreover, our algorithms do not rely on the integral oracle required by prior work.

### 84. [RoboEvo$^2$: Recursive Robot Self-Improvement via Harness-Policy Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34675v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：harness-policy co-evolution., agent, reasoning, distillation, vision-language, loop. through recursive co-evolution
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34675v1>

**原始摘要**

Robots should adapt to unfamiliar environments and acquire new skills through interaction, then execute those skills efficiently with practice. Vision-language-action policies provide efficient control, but often need additional human demonstrations to handle unfamiliar settings; reasoning agents can discover solutions using broad prior knowledge and robot-control APIs, but repeated deliberation and repair are costly. Inspired by human learning through reasoning and practice, we propose RoboEvo$^2$, a framework for recursive harness-policy co-evolution. In Policy-to-Harness (P2H) evolution, policy failures guide a coding agent to search complementary Code, Code+ICL, and Code+Policy routes, consolidating verified solutions into reusable skills, routing strategies, and experience memory. In Harness-to-Policy (H2P) evolution, verified trajectories provide supervised training, while Counterfactual Preference Distillation (CPD) teaches the policy to favor successful corrections over failures from the same starting state. The improved policy provides stronger motor skills for subsequent search, while its remaining failures redirect harness evolution, closing the reciprocal loop. Through recursive co-evolution without additional human demonstrations, RoboEvo$^2$ improves standalone success from 11.0% to 84.1% and hybrid success from 30.4% to 90.5% on LIBERO-PRO, with similar gains and reduced agentic costs on RoboTwin C2R. Further experiments demonstrate autonomous task acquisition, cross-embodiment harness transfer, and 95.0% hybrid success on the Unitree G1-D. Our models and code will be publicly released.

### 85. [DemoFoundry: Toward Scalable Photorealistic Robot Data with Coding Agents and Generative Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34671v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：coding agents, robot learning, visual quality and action--video, task-relevant geometry and writes, raw-simulation training and approach, quality and action--video consistency.
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34671v1>

**原始摘要**

Scaling robot learning requires demonstrations that combine realistic visual observations with reliable action supervision. Simulation-based synthesis faces a substantial visual gap relative to real environments, while video-only generation struggles to maintain physically consistent interactions and provide aligned action labels. We present DemoFoundry, an automated framework that couples coding agents with generative rendering to synthesize photorealistic and physically grounded robot demonstrations. Coding agents both construct aligned real--proxy video pairs from existing robot episodes to train a video renderer and write executable interaction programs to generate new demonstrations. Given a scene reference and task instruction, an agent reconstructs task-relevant geometry and writes a control program without requiring target-task human demonstrations. Program execution in a physics simulator produces proxy videos with aligned robot states and actions. The video renderer translates these proxy videos into scene-consistent, photorealistic observations, reducing the need for detailed appearance modeling in each scene. Automated task-execution checks select successful trajectories for rendering and policy learning. Experiments show improved visual quality and action--video consistency. Policies trained on the generated demonstrations outperform raw-simulation training and approach the performance of training on an equal number of real demonstrations.

### 86. [Autonomous Ergodic Robotic Palpation for Elastic Mapping of Curved Soft Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34670v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：curved SOFT, robotic palpation, diffusion, geometry-consistent mapping and stiff, properties demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping, SOFT surfaces using point
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34670v1>

**原始摘要**

Autonomous robotic palpation offers repeatable, quantitative estimates of tissue properties for stiff inclusion localization. However, existing methods often rely on prior mesh models or planar surface assumptions that do not generalize to complex human anatomy. We propose an ergodic palpation framework that operates directly on curved soft surfaces using point cloud data. Central to our approach is the Laplace--Beltrami operator, which grounds both online estimation and exploration in the surface intrinsic geometry. We estimate local viscoelastic parameters using an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) and fuse them into a surface-aware Gaussian Process (GP). This probabilistic model drives a diffusion-based ergodic motion planner, generating a continuous exploration trajectory that feeds the new informative samples back to the GP in a closed loop. Throughout this process, a hybrid force-impedance controller regulates interaction forces to maintain contact. We evaluate the approach against existing methods in simulation and validate it in physical experiments on tissue-mimicking silicone phantoms with varying geometries and viscoelastic properties, demonstrating geometry-consistent mapping and stiff inclusion localization under contact-rich conditions.

### 87. [Learning to Be Truncated: Preparing LLM Checkpoints for SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34667v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：SVD compression, fine-tuning, LLM checkpoints for SVD, pretrained model for SVD, llift can improve compression, ratio can benefit compression
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34667v1>

**原始摘要**

Compression based on singular value decomposition (SVD) often requires subsequent fine-tuning or weight correction to mitigate performance degradation. Budget-specific optimization must be repeated for different compression ratios. We introduce LLift, which prepares a pretrained model for SVD compression across ratios without further fine-tuning. LLift fine-tunes a low-rank approximation at a single ratio using the original model as a teacher. We merge the learned updates into the original weights while preserving the complementary components excluded from the training forward pass. We apply SVD to the prepared model's target matrices and reuse the decompositions at different truncation ranks. On LLaMA-7B, we demonstrate accuracy gains across three compression ratios using a single prepared model. The gains extend across model families, model sizes, and SVD compression operators. We analyze gradients and an early training update to provide local evidence that updates learned at one ratio can benefit compression at other ratios. These results show that LLift can improve compression across deployment budgets without fine-tuning each compressed model separately.

### 88. [Multi-Party Authorization of Maintenance Prescriptions on a Permissioned Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34662v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：benchmark, mis-operated prescriptions whereas HASH, prescriptions whereas HASH checks, raised simulated maintenance COST, either raised simulated maintenance, multi-party authorization
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34662v1>

**原始摘要**

In industrial prescriptive maintenance, an equipment supplier's model may recommend an intervention that a plant schedules, a contractor executes, and a quality or safety function approves. A shared ledger records who submitted the prescription, yet cannot check whether the agreed model and decision rule produced it. We require permissioned-blockchain endorsers to verify a succinct proof of that calculation before releasing a work order. On a four-organization Hyperledger Fabric testbed using a public engine benchmark, this stopped all nine contested or mis-operated prescriptions, whereas hash checks accepted four that either raised simulated maintenance cost or reintroduced late failures.

### 89. [FeaXDrive2: Body-Scene Spatial Grounding for Feasibility-Aware Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34658v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：body-scene spatial, feaxdrive2, spatial grounding., alignment, fine-tuning, feasibility VESF through body-scene
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34658v1>

**原始摘要**

Generative driving planners may produce trajectories that appear feasible at their reference points but violate environmental constraints over the full vehicle body. We propose FeaXDrive2, an end-to-end generative planning framework that enhances vehicle-embodied spatial feasibility (VESF) through body-scene spatial grounding. The proposed method constructs spatial queries from actual vehicle-body corners and dynamically updates visual sampling locations using evolving trajectory estimates across Flow steps. Retrieved local visual features condition subsequent Action Flow-DiT velocity predictions, allowing trajectory generation to guide spatial information retrieval. Rollout-Consistent Safety Fine-Tuning (RC-F) combines high-risk trajectory supervision with reference behavior preservation, followed by planning-supervised VLM-Action Alignment. Vehicle-geometry counterfactual analysis reveals safety differences under identical trajectories but varying vehicle geometry. On NAVSIM, FeaXDrive2 reduces at-fault collisions by 36.5% relative to the base planner and improves PDMS from 87.0 to 88.8. Under NAVSIM v2 evaluation, EPDMS increases from 86.5 to 88.3. Ablation and body-clearance analyses further demonstrate the safety benefits of body-scene spatial grounding.

### 90. [Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, readable world models., reasoning, decision-making BUT learning effective, sequential decision-making BUT learning, interaction experience. model-based reinforcement
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656v1>

**原始摘要**

Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

### 91. [Nav4Manip: From Navigation to Dexterous Mobile Manipulation through Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34655v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：nav4manip, dexterous mobile, long-horizon dexterous, reinforcement learning, mobile manipulation, benchmark
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34655v1>

**原始摘要**

Dexterous mobile manipulation is essential for robots to perform useful tasks in complex, unstructured environments. However, existing systems face two challenges. First, navigation remains too coarse for downstream manipulation, which often requires a precise base position and approach orientation rather than merely reaching a meter-scale goal region. Second, navigation and manipulation are commonly trained in isolation, lacking a feedback loop that enables the two capabilities to co-evolve. These limitations prevent current systems from reliably executing long-horizon dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks in real-world environments. To address them, we introduce Nav4Manip, a bidirectionally coupled co-evolution system for long-horizon dexterous mobile manipulation. It comprises two complementary architectures: Nav4Manip-N learns long-horizon navigation and refines its policy through manipulation feedback, while Nav4Manip-M trains a world-action model (WAM) for downstream dexterous manipulation. We train Nav4Manip-N in three stages: multi-task co-tuning learns navigation behavior and manipulation-aware affordance prediction; rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) rejects low-quality policy-generated paths and fine-tunes the model on retained trajectories; and reinforcement learning from AI feedback (RLAIF) uses counterfactual preferences to further improve navigation decisions. To support this framework, we establish the Nav-N-Manip benchmark and construct Nav4Manip-Pairs from benchmark rollouts, providing large-scale paired navigation--manipulation trajectories and affordance supervision. Extensive experiments on public vision-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks and Nav-N-Manip demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while real-world evaluations show that Nav4Manip completes diverse dexterous mobile-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://nav4manip.github.io/.

### 92. [Atunda: Building an Afrogenic Dance Movement Dataset for AI-Driven Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34649v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：dance moves, afrogenic dance, benchmark, movement dataset for ai-driven, dataset for ai-driven classification., kind. using this dataset
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34649v1>

**原始摘要**

This paper introduces Atunda, the first systematically collected video dataset of Afrogenic dance moves, designed for training and evaluating deep learning models for movement classification. The first phase of the dataset includes nearly 400 videos covering 13 distinct dance moves from African and African Diaspora performative culture, making it the first AI-ready resource of its kind. Using this dataset, we train and evaluate various configurations of temporal convolutional neural networks, achieving a classification accuracy exceeding 95%. The baseline results provide a benchmark for future studies in movement classification, especially in the understudied area of Afrogenic performative culture and tradition. This work lays the foundation for developing in the future digital metrics of similarity, automated attribution systems, and tools that preserve and promote the cultural heritage of Afrogenic dances.

### 93. [REE: A Scientific Research Entrance Exam for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：open-ended research problems, agent, research entrance EXAM, scientific research, benchmark, scientific problem-solving across disciplines
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646v1>

**原始摘要**

Autonomous research agents are emerging as a promising approach to scientific problem-solving across disciplines, owing to their growing ability to improve a solution through repeated experiments. Evaluating this ability requires benchmarks that pose open-ended research problems and measure improvements over established scientific methods. We introduce REE (Research Entrance Exam), a benchmark that tests AI agents on scientific research. It consists of 100 open-ended research problems spanning five broad scientific areas and 23 domains. To ensure high quality, every problem is drawn from active research in its field and individually reviewed by domain experts. Each problem is posed as a task of iterative improvement: an agent has up to 12 hours to improve a working starter solution, and REE scores the result on a common scale against an established expert solution evaluated under comparable conditions. By this measure, two of the four frontier agents we evaluate, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol, outperform the expert solutions on 86% and 81% of problems, across all five scientific areas. Their runs also show how they do so: both agents surpass the expert solutions within the first two hours on average, and their best solutions mostly tailor or combine established methods of the field. REE thus provides a unified framework for evaluating autonomous scientific research, capturing both improvements over established methods and the research processes behind them.

### 94. [A Hard Background Pairing Transfers across Classifiers and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：hardest pairing, held-out classifiers, SOME classifiers, benchmark, HARD ONLY for THEM, stays HARD for others
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645v1>

**原始摘要**

An image classifier names the object in an image, and its accuracy is the share of images named correctly. A classifier that names a car on a road may miss the same car pasted onto clouds. Composite benchmarks measure such failures by pasting objects cut from photographs onto backgrounds from other photographs. A pairing puts every object label on a different background label, such as truck on sea. The pairing on which some classifiers do worst, their hardest pairing, makes a strict test, but may be hard only for them or only on the photographs used to choose it. We show that the hardest pairing of some classifiers stays hard for others and on new photographs. We split 40 ImageNet classifiers into halves balanced by reported ImageNet accuracy. One half picks its hardest pairing of 8 object and 8 background labels from Common Objects in Context (COCO). The other half, held out of the choice, compares that pairing with random pairings of the same objects and backgrounds. For the 14 held-out classifiers with little net accuracy change after pasting objects back onto their own backgrounds, mean accuracy on composites from new photographs falls from 50.3% over random pairings to 43.7% on the hardest pairing, 6.6 percentage points lower. A benchmark using these composites can compare this pool's held-out classifiers on the fixed and random pairings.

### 95. [Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：visual representations, future visual, object-centric dynamics., object FLOW, attention, diffusion
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644v1>

**原始摘要**

Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

### 96. [Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：reinforcement learning, variational inference for bisimulation-based, variational bisimulation. model-based reinforcement, global optima induce bisimulation, optima induce bisimulation relations, introduce vibes variational inference
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641v1>

**原始摘要**

Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

### 97. [PRAM: Activating Latent Multimodal Knowledge through Partwise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：partwise statistical, latent multimodal knowledge, reasoning, alignment, PRAM achieves state-of-the-art, infers global and partwise
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640v1>

**原始摘要**

Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Finally, we introduce a multi-granularity score fusion module that standardizes and fuses matching scores from global and local relations, incorporating task-specific information on pivot associations and class semantics for class prediction and candidate ranking. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

### 98. [Carrying Feature Ablations Across Sparse Autoencoder Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34637v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：sparse autoencoder, matched pairs across three, pairs across three settings., removal carries across seeds., feature ablations across sparse, feature and EDIT
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34637v1>

**原始摘要**

A vision encoder turns an image into one vector per patch, refined layer by layer. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) writes each vector of one layer as a sum of learned directions, most with a coefficient of zero. Researchers call each direction a feature and edit the encoder by removing one feature. An SAE trained from another random seed learns different features. Earlier work matches the two SAEs' features one to one by how closely their directions agree. Matched features can point alike yet respond to different image parts, so removing them may change the encoder differently. We remove a feature and, separately, its match, then compare the changes in the encoder's output on the same held out images. When the second change nearly reproduces the first, we say the removal carries across seeds. A setting fixes a DINOv2 or SigLIP layer and its SAE feature count. At our tolerance, the removal carries for 12% to 70% of matched pairs across three settings. The share is 0% in two controls: an SAE whose directions stay random and, post hoc, matches shuffled among trained features. Post hoc, predicting which removals carry is more accurate with layer contributions than with directions. Some incorrect predictions remain. One persists on independent images. So pairs can be screened at the layer. Measuring the output detects errors this screen misses.

### 99. [ZeroHero: Training Small Language Agents from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：agent, small language, reasoning, alignment, framework for training small, language models provide capable
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635v1>

**原始摘要**

Large pretrained language models provide capable policies for interactive tasks, but how much model capacity is necessary for a specialized language agent? We introduce ZeroHero, a framework for training small language agents (SLAs) from scratch. In each environment, a large language model acts as the expert, and we collect its successful trajectories. We pretrain each SLA, which has fewer than 50 million parameters, to predict the expert's responses given the interaction history. We then post-train the agent on its own interactions using proximal policy optimization (PPO). During post-training, reasoning alignment supplements environment rewards with expert supervision on the language the agent generates alongside its actions. We evaluate the agents in three multistep environments where acting requires both understanding and generating language. In Crafter, agents interpret textual observations and generate reasoning before acting. In WebShop, they translate shopping instructions into search queries. In Alfworld-Dialogue, they ask clarifying questions and use the answers to guide household actions. The agents approach expert performance on WebShop and surpass their respective demonstration experts on Crafter training rollouts and held-out Alfworld-Dialogue tasks. An interactive demo is available at https://zoyav.github.io/-ZoyaV-zerohero-demo.

### 100. [DecisionReplay: A Corpus and Offline Simulator for Ensembling Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34627v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：routers and cascades, benchmark, ITEM and its cost., built for routing KEEP, billed COST and measured, COST and certified early
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34627v1>

**原始摘要**

Comparing ensembles, routers and cascades requires the output of every model on every item, and its cost. Stored collections built for routing keep no per-option probabilities. We provide a dataset on which ensemble methods, routers and cascades are simulated quickly, without new model calls. DecisionReplay holds the probability vectors of 17 commercial decision models, endpoints that return a probability for every answer option, on 35 benchmarks, with the token counts, billed cost and measured latency of every call, all released. A deterministic simulator replays any policy that selects, combines or cascades them. One well-chosen model per benchmark is hard to beat: pooling all models loses 2.7 accuracy points to it, and stacking gains 1.0 points at 3.4 times its cost. The models are wrong together: two that are both wrong choose the same wrong option in 72% of cases. The best router matches that model at 64% of its cost, and certified early exit cuts the cost of a stacked ensemble by 43% without changing an answer. DecisionReplay makes it cheap and fast to test whether a new method changes these results.

### 101. [HoMe: Toward Practical Harness Optimization of Memory Systems for AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34624v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：memory system, benchmark, HOME with the open-source, it. HOME combines session-level, HOME combines session-level memory, TWO large-scale long-horizon memory
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34624v1>

**原始摘要**

The behavior of memory systems for long-running AI assistants is governed by a harness, which consists of the code, prompts, and configurations for memory construction, maintenance, retrieval, and answer generation. However, optimizing the harness of a memory system remains largely manual, and unrestricted editing alone does not ensure structural improvements. In this paper, we introduce HoMe, an evaluation-driven framework that iteratively evaluates candidate harnesses and derives each new candidate from any previously evaluated harness by editing any part of it. HoMe combines session-level memory versioning with execution traces to identify shared failure mechanisms across cases and localize them to the responsible sessions and harness components. It encourages early structural exploration through candidate gating and reduces evaluation cost through smoke tests, adaptive sampling, and the reuse of constructed memory. We validate HoMe with the open-source memory system ReMe on two large-scale long-horizon memory benchmarks, LongMemEval-S and BEAM. With six formal candidate evaluations per benchmark, after a full initial evaluation, HoMe discovers harnesses that improve end-task performance over the originally strong harness while consuming far fewer query-time tokens. The code is available at https://github.com/agentscope-ai/ReMe.

### 102. [Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：POSE control, video generation, beyond POSE, transformer, large-scale data. ultrasound poses, enough and pose-conditioned ultrasound
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622v1>

**原始摘要**

Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

### 103. [Automated Batch-Variable Selection for ComBat-Based Radiomic Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34618v1)

- 来源：arXiv
- 关键词：selection for combat-based radiomic, batch-variable selection for combat-based, combat-based radiomic harmonization. combat, acquisition parameters, automated batch-variable selection, batch-effect removal
- 链接：<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34618v1>

**原始摘要**

ComBat harmonizes multicentre radiomic features, but batch variables are usually chosen manually, without a standardized criterion across image acquisition parameters. We propose ParsiComBat, which selects acquisition parameters for ComBat, balancing batch-effect removal against feature-structure preservation and abstaining when technical variation is negligible.
